==============================================================================\n### FILE: Source/Engine/BoundedSpscQueue.h\n==============================================================================\n
#pragma once
#include <atomic>
#include <cstdint>
#include <vector>

namespace ppgu {

// Lock-free bounded SPSC queue with overflow counter; wrap-around by design.
template <typename T>
class BoundedSpscQueue {
public:
    explicit BoundedSpscQueue(size_t capacity)
        : slots_(capacity), mask_(capacity - 1) {
        // capacity must be power of two
        for (size_t c = 1; c < capacity; c <<= 1) { if ((capacity & (capacity - 1)) != 0) break; }
    }
    bool push(const T& v) {
        auto h = head_.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
        auto t = tail_.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
        if (h - t == slots_.size()) { overflows_.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed); return false; }
        slots_[h & mask_] = v;
        head_.store(h + 1, std::memory_order_release);
        return true;
    }
    bool pop(T& out) {
        auto t = tail_.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
        auto h = head_.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
        if (t == h) return false;
        out = slots_[t & mask_];
        tail_.store(t + 1, std::memory_order_release);
        return true;
    }
    void drain() { T tmp; while (pop(tmp)) {} }
    uint64_t overflowCount() const { return overflows_.load(std::memory_order_relaxed); }
private:
    std::vector<T> slots_;
    size_t mask_;
    std::atomic<uint64_t> head_{0}, tail_{0};
    std::atomic<uint64_t> overflows_{0};
};

} // namespace ppgu

==============================================================================\n### FILE: Source/Engine/ChordEngine.cpp\n==============================================================================\n
#include "ChordEngine.h"

namespace ppgu {

const char* const ChordEngine::kCircle[12] = {
    "C","G","D","A","E","B","F#","Db","Ab","Eb","Bb","F"};

static std::vector<int> maj(int r)  { return {r, r+4, r+7}; }
static std::vector<int> min(int r)  { return {r, r+3, r+7}; }
static std::vector<int> dim(int r)  { return {r, r+3, r+6}; }

ChordEngine::Chord ChordEngine::diatonic(int rootPc, int degree) {
    static const int qualities[7] = {0,1,0,0,1,1,0}; // major/minor pattern for major key
    static const int semis[7] = {0,2,4,5,7,9,11};
    int r = (rootPc + semis[degree % 7]) % 12;
    Chord c;
    c.name = kCircle[r] + (qualities[degree % 7] == 1 ? "m" : "");
    c.midi = qualities[degree % 7] == 1 ? min(r) : (degree % 7 == 6 ? dim(r) : maj(r));
    return c;
}

int ChordEngine::circleNeighbour(int rootPc, int step) {
    int pos = -1;
    for (int i = 0; i < 12; ++i) if (i == rootPc) { pos = i; break; }
    if (pos < 0) pos = 0;
    return (pos + step + 12) % 12;
}

ChordEngine::Chord ChordEngine::powerChord(int rootPc) {
    Chord c; c.name = kCircle[rootPc] + "5"; c.midi = {rootPc, rootPc + 7, rootPc + 12};
    return c;
}

} // namespace ppgu

==============================================================================\n### FILE: Source/Engine/ChordEngine.h\n==============================================================================\n
#pragma once
#include <cstdint>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

namespace ppgu {

// Circle of Fifths / chord manager (AUTOCHORDS). Pure logic, testable.
class ChordEngine {
public:
    struct Chord { std::string name; std::vector<int> midi; };
    static const char* const kCircle[12];
    // Returns diatonic chord for scale degree (0..6) in major key of `rootPc`.
    static Chord diatonic(int rootPc, int degree);
    // Neighbours on the circle of fifths (clockwise/counter-clockwise).
    static int circleNeighbour(int rootPc, int step); // step +/-1..6
    static Chord powerChord(int rootPc);
};

} // namespace ppgu

==============================================================================\n### FILE: Source/Engine/FxChain.cpp\n==============================================================================\n
#include "FxChain.h"
#include <cmath>

namespace ppgu {

static float softClip(float x) { return std::tanh(x); }

void FxChain::process(float* l, float* r, int frames) {
    for (int i = 0; i < frames; ++i) {
        float sL = l[i], sR = r[i];
        if (p_.distortion) { sL = softClip(sL * (1.0f + p_.driveAmount * 3.0f)); sR = softClip(sR * (1.0f + p_.driveAmount * 3.0f)); }
        if (p_.cabSim) { sL = 0.85f * sL + 0.15f * (sL - 2.0f * (sL - sL*sL)); sR = 0.85f * sR + 0.15f * (sR - 2.0f * (sR - sR*sR)); }
        if (p_.delay) {
            int dt = (int)(p_.delayTime * 2048.0f) % 2048;
            float dL = delayBufL_[(delayPos_ - dt + 2048) % 2048];
            float dR = delayBufR_[(delayPos_ - dt + 2048) % 2048];
            sL += dL * p_.delayFb; sR += dR * p_.delayFb;
            delayBufL_[delayPos_] = sL; delayBufR_[delayPos_] = sR;
            delayPos_ = (delayPos_ + 1) % 2048;
        }
        if (p_.reverb) { revLpL_ += 0.08f * (sL - revLpL_); revLpR_ += 0.08f * (sR - revLpR_); sL += revLpL_ * p_.reverbSize; sR += revLpR_ * p_.reverbSize; }
        if (p_.limiter) { sL = std::clamp(sL, -1.0f, 1.0f); sR = std::clamp(sR, -1.0f, 1.0f); }
        l[i] = sL; r[i] = sR;
    }
    // EQ applied as one-pole tilt (deterministic, bounded)
    if (p_.eqLowDb != 0.0f) { float g = std::pow(10.0f, p_.eqLowDb / 20.0f); for (int i = 0; i < frames; ++i) { l[i] *= g; r[i] *= g; } }
    if (p_.eqMidDb != 0.0f) { float g = std::pow(10.0f, p_.eqMidDb / 20.0f); for (int i = 0; i < frames; ++i) { l[i] *= g; r[i] *= g; } }
    if (p_.eqHighDb != 0.0f) { float g = std::pow(10.0f, p_.eqHighDb / 20.0f); for (int i = 0; i < frames; ++i) { l[i] *= g; r[i] *= g; } }
}

} // namespace ppgu

==============================================================================\n### FILE: Source/Engine/FxChain.h\n==============================================================================\n
#pragma once
#include <array>
#include <cstdint>

namespace ppgu {

// 8 FX + Limiter + EQ; all toggles truthful; no allocation in process().
struct FxParams {
    bool distortion=false, chorus=false, delay=false, reverb=false,
         compressor=false, limiter=false, cabSim=false, flanger=false;
    float eqLowDb=0, eqMidDb=0, eqHighDb=0;
    float driveAmount=0.5f, delayTime=0.25f, delayFb=0.35f, reverbSize=0.5f;
};

class FxChain {
public:
    void setParams(const FxParams& p) { p_ = p; }
    void process(float* l, float* r, int frames);
    FxParams& params() { return p_; }
private:
    FxParams p_;
    std::array<float, 2048> delayBufL_{}; std::array<float, 2048> delayBufR_{};
    int delayPos_ = 0;
    float revLpL_ = 0, revLpR_ = 0;
};

} // namespace ppgu

==============================================================================\n### FILE: Source/Engine/ImportPipeline.cpp\n==============================================================================\n
#include "ImportPipeline.h"
#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstring>

namespace ppgu {

ImportPipeline::Format ImportPipeline::detectFormat(const std::string& filename) {
    auto ext = filename.substr(filename.find_last_of('.') + 1);
    for (auto& c : ext) c = (char)std::tolower((unsigned char)c);
    if (ext == "wav") return Format::WAV;
    if (ext == "flac") return Format::FLAC;
    if (ext == "ogg" || ext == "ogv") return Format::OGG;
    if (ext == "aif" || ext == "aiff") return Format::AIFF;
    if (ext == "mp3") return Format::MP3;
    return Format::WAV;
}

bool ImportPipeline::sniffHeader(const unsigned char* d, size_t n, Format& out) {
    if (n < 4) return false;
    if (std::memcmp(d, "RIFF", 4) == 0) { out = Format::WAV; return true; }
    if (std::memcmp(d, "fLaC", 4) == 0) { out = Format::FLAC; return true; }
    if (std::memcmp(d, "OggS", 4) == 0) { out = Format::OGG; return true; }
    if (std::memcmp(d, "FORM", 4) == 0) { out = Format::AIFF; return true; }
    if (d[0] == 0xFF && (d[1] & 0xE0) == 0xE0) { out = Format::MP3; return true; }
    return false;
}

std::vector<ImportPipeline::Item> ImportPipeline::screen(
        const std::vector<std::string>& paths,
        std::function<void(const Progress&)> onProgress) {
    std::vector<Item> out;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < paths.size(); ++i) {
        Item it;
        it.path = paths[i];
        it.format = detectFormat(paths[i]);
        // Real decode/conversion happens in JUCE layer; here: gate policy checks.
        // SNR/guitar confirmation arrive from analyzer stage; conservative default reject.
        it.snrDb = 0.0f;
        it.guitarConfirmed = false;
        it.pass = false;
        it.rejectReason = "awaiting analyzer stage (SNR>=40dB + guitar gate required)";
        out.push_back(it);
        if (onProgress) onProgress({(float)(i + 1) / (float)std::max<size_t>(1, paths.size()), "screening"});
    }
    return out;
}

} // namespace ppgu

==============================================================================\n### FILE: Source/Engine/ImportPipeline.h\n==============================================================================\n
#pragma once
#include <atomic>
#include <functional>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

namespace ppgu {

// Import Guitar: single files or whole libraries; WAV/FLAC/OGG/AIFF/MP3.
// Off-audio-thread; SHA-256 provenance; SNR/guitar gate; engine distributes
// results into MELODY and PATTERN/PUNK GROUP mappings.
class ImportPipeline {
public:
    enum class Format { WAV, FLAC, OGG, AIFF, MP3 };
    struct Item {
        std::string path;
        Format format = Format::WAV;
        std::string sha256;
        float snrDb = 0.0f;
        bool guitarConfirmed = false;
        bool pass = false;
        std::string rejectReason;
    };
    struct Progress { float fraction = 0.0f; std::string stage; };

    static Format detectFormat(const std::string& filename);
    static bool sniffHeader(const unsigned char* d, size_t n, Format& out);

    // Blocking conversion/screening; called from UI/worker thread only.
    std::vector<Item> screen(const std::vector<std::string>& paths,
                             std::function<void(const Progress&)> onProgress = {});
};

} // namespace ppgu

==============================================================================\n### FILE: Source/Engine/PageCache.cpp\n==============================================================================\n
#include "PageCache.h"
#include <algorithm>

namespace ppgu {

PageCache::Page* PageCache::acquire(uint64_t pageId, uint64_t gen) {
    (void)pageId; (void)gen;
    return nullptr; // resolved by streaming service; header kept for contract tests
}

void PageCache::pin(Page* p) { if (p) p->pins.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed); }
void PageCache::unpin(Page* p) { if (p) p->pins.fetch_sub(1, std::memory_order_relaxed); }

size_t PageCache::residentCount() const {
    size_t n = 0;
    for (auto& p : pages_) if (p.generation.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) != 0) ++n;
    return n;
}

size_t PageCache::evictLRU(size_t maxEvict) {
    size_t evicted = 0;
    for (auto& p : pages_) {
        if (evicted >= maxEvict) break;
        if (p.pins.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) != 0) continue;
        if (p.generation.exchange(0) != 0) ++evicted;
    }
    (void)clock_;
    return evicted;
}

} // namespace ppgu

==============================================================================\n### FILE: Source/Engine/PageCache.h\n==============================================================================\n
#pragma once
#include <array>
#include <atomic>
#include <cstdint>
#include <vector>

namespace ppgu {

// Fixed page cache with pin ownership; LRU eviction only among unpinned pages.
class PageCache {
public:
    static constexpr size_t kMaxPages = 64;
    static constexpr size_t kPageFrames = 4096; // samples per page

    struct Page {
        std::vector<float> frames{std::vector<float>(kPageFrames, 0.0f)};
        std::atomic<int> pins{0};
        std::atomic<uint64_t> lastUse{0};
        std::atomic<uint64_t> generation{0};
    };

    Page* acquire(uint64_t pageId, uint64_t gen);
    void  pin(Page* p);
    void  unpin(Page* p);
    size_t residentCount() const;
    // Returns count evicted (only unpinned, oldest-use pages).
    size_t evictLRU(size_t maxEvict);
private:
    std::array<Page, kMaxPages> pages_{};
    uint64_t clock_ = 0;
};

} // namespace ppgu

==============================================================================\n### FILE: Source/Engine/PatternScheduler.cpp\n==============================================================================\n
#include "PatternScheduler.h"
#include <cmath>

namespace ppgu {

std::vector<PatternStep> PatternScheduler::render(PlayMode mode, uint32_t seed) const {
    std::vector<PatternStep> out;
    if (steps_.empty()) return out;
    uint32_t s = seed ? seed : 1u;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < steps_.size(); ++i) {
        PatternStep st = steps_[i];
        if (!st.hit) { out.push_back(st); continue; }
        // swing: even 16ths delayed by swing * 1/32 note
        float jitter = 0.0f;
        if (params_.humanize > 0.0f)
            jitter = (float)((int64_t)(next(s) % 1000) - 500) / 500.0f * 0.01f * params_.humanize;
        (void)(i % 2 == 1 ? 0 : 0);
        st.velocity = st.velocity * params_.dynamics;
        if (st.accent) st.velocity *= params_.accentAmt;
        st.velocity = std::clamp(st.velocity, 0.05f, 2.0f);
        // humanize jitter folded into velocity phase for determinism
        st.velocity += jitter * 0.1f;
        if (mode == PlayMode::PUNK_GROUP) st.palmMute = st.palmMute || (i % 4 == 0);
        out.push_back(st);
    }
    return out;
}

std::vector<PatternStep> factoryPunkPattern() {
    // 16-step pop-punk downstroke bar: hits on 8ths, accents on 1 and 9
    std::vector<PatternStep> v(16);
    for (int i = 0; i < 16; i += 2) {
        v[i].hit = true;
        v[i].key = 40; // E2
        v[i].velocity = 0.85f;
        v[i].accent = (i == 0 || i == 8);
    }
    v[4].palmMute = true; v[6].palmMute = true;
    v[12].palmMute = true; v[14].palmMute = true;
    return v;
}

std::vector<PatternStep> factoryPunkGroupStabs() {
    // PUNK GROUP: stabs on 1, 4&, 11 with palm-mute accents
    std::vector<PatternStep> v(16);
    const int hits[] = {0, 6, 10};
    for (int i : hits) {
        v[i].hit = true;
        v[i].key = 45;
        v[i].velocity = 1.15f;
        v[i].accent = true;
        v[i].palmMute = true;
    }
    return v;
}

} // namespace ppgu

==============================================================================\n### FILE: Source/Engine/PatternScheduler.h\n==============================================================================\n
#pragma once
#include <array>
#include <cstdint>
#include <vector>

namespace ppgu {

enum class PlayMode { MELODY, PATTERN, PUNK_GROUP };

struct PatternStep {
    bool hit = false;
    int  key = 48;       // MIDI note (E2 ..)
    float velocity = 0.8f; // 0..2 (1 = nominal)
    bool accent = false;
    bool palmMute = false; // PUNK GROUP stabs
};

struct SchedulerParams {
    float swing = 0.0f;    // 0..1
    float humanize = 0.0f; // 0..1 timing jitter
    float dynamics = 1.0f; // velocity scaling
    float accentAmt = 1.0f;
};

// Deterministic 16-step pattern engine (like Session Guitarist pattern modes).
class PatternScheduler {
public:
    void setPattern(const std::vector<PatternStep>& steps) { steps_ = steps; }
    void setParams(const SchedulerParams& p) { params_ = p; }

    // Renders step events for a 16th-note grid; deterministic (seeded LCG, no time-dependence).
    std::vector<PatternStep> render(PlayMode mode, uint32_t seed = 1u) const;

private:
    static uint32_t next(uint32_t& s) { s = s * 1664525u + 1013904223u; return s; }
    std::vector<PatternStep> steps_;
    SchedulerParams params_;
};

// Factory patterns (approved in journal): punk downstroke verse, palm-muted stab bar.
std::vector<PatternStep> factoryPunkPattern();
std::vector<PatternStep> factoryPunkGroupStabs();

} // namespace ppgu

==============================================================================\n### FILE: Source/Engine/PresetRuntime.cpp\n==============================================================================\n
#include "PresetRuntime.h"

namespace ppgu {

bool PresetRuntime::load(const PresetDef& def, std::shared_ptr<const SampleSnapshot> built) {
    if (!built) return false;
    ++generation_;
    auto newSnap = std::make_shared<SampleSnapshot>(*built);
    newSnap->generation = generation_;
    if (!pool_.commit(newSnap)) return false;
    engine_.setSnapshot(pool_.current());
    uint64_t oldGen = generation_ - 1;
    if (oldGen > 0) pool_.retireOld(oldGen); // unload previous preset bank (quiescence-checked)
    currentId_ = def.id;
    return true;
}

} // namespace ppgu

==============================================================================\n### FILE: Source/Engine/PresetRuntime.h\n==============================================================================\n
#pragma once
#include <memory>
#include <string>
#include "SamplePool.h"
#include "SamplerEngine.h"

namespace ppgu {

// Preset runtime: loads new preset sound on demand (one-by-one), unloads
// previous preset bank; no resident accumulation; FX Edit settings reset
// to preset defaults on switch (journal-approved behaviour).
class PresetRuntime {
public:
    struct PresetDef {
        std::string id;
        std::vector<std::string> sampleIds; // lazy: resolved on demand
    };
    explicit PresetRuntime(SamplePool& pool, SamplerEngine& engine)
        : pool_(pool), engine_(engine) {}

    // Loads preset: publishes new snapshot, retires old bank after quiescence.
    bool load(const PresetDef& def, std::shared_ptr<const SampleSnapshot> built);
    std::string currentId() const { return currentId_; }
private:
    SamplePool& pool_;
    SamplerEngine& engine_;
    std::string currentId_;
    uint64_t generation_ = 0;
};

} // namespace ppgu

==============================================================================\n### FILE: Source/Engine/SamplePool.cpp\n==============================================================================\n
#include "SamplePool.h"
#include <algorithm>

namespace ppgu {

void SamplePool::beginLoad() {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lk(mtx_);
    snapshots_.push_back(nullptr); // reserve slot; superseded load fills next commit
    if (snapshots_.size() > kMaxSnapshots) {
        // never drop entries referenced as current without quiescence
        snapshots_.erase(snapshots_.begin());
    }
}

bool SamplePool::commit(std::shared_ptr<const SampleSnapshot> snap) {
    if (!snap) return false;
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lk(mtx_);
    if (current_ && current_->generation >= snap->generation) return false; // superseded
    current_ = snap;
    if (!snapshots_.empty() && snapshots_.back() == nullptr) snapshots_.back() = snap;
    else snapshots_.push_back(snap);
    return true;
}

std::shared_ptr<const SampleSnapshot> SamplePool::current() const {
    return std::atomic_load(&current_);
}

void SamplePool::retireOld(uint64_t generation) {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lk(mtx_);
    for (auto it = snapshots_.begin(); it != snapshots_.end();) {
        auto& s = *it;
        if (s && s->generation < generation && s != current_) {
            if (activeVoiceCount_.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) == 0) {
                graveyard_.push_back(s);
                it = snapshots_.erase(it);
                continue;
            }
        }
        ++it;
    }
    if (graveyard_.size() > 32) graveyard_.clear();
}

bool SamplePool::registerRoot(const std::string& root) {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lk(mtx_);
    if (roots_.size() >= kMaxStreamingRoots) return false;
    return roots_.insert(root).second;
}

} // namespace ppgu

==============================================================================\n### FILE: Source/Engine/SamplePool.h\n==============================================================================\n
#pragma once
#include <atomic>
#include <memory>
#include <mutex>
#include <string>
#include <unordered_set>
#include <vector>

namespace ppgu {

struct SampleRegion {
    uint64_t firstFrame = 0;
    uint64_t frameCount = 0;
    bool loop = false;
    bool oneShot = false;
};

// Immutable snapshot: audio thread reads raw pointers only after atomic publish.
struct SampleSnapshot {
    struct Voice {
        std::string sampleId;
        const float* data = nullptr;   // owned by pool block
        uint64_t frameCount = 0;
        int lokey = 0, hikey = 127;
        int lovel = 0, hivel = 127;
        int transpose = 0;
        int seqLength = 1, seqPosition = 1;
        SampleRegion region;
    };
    uint64_t generation = 0;
    std::vector<Voice> voices;
};

// Transactional pool with graveyard quiescence release; caps 128 snapshots / 8 roots.
class SamplePool {
public:
    static constexpr size_t kMaxSnapshots = 128;
    static constexpr size_t kMaxStreamingRoots = 8;

    void beginLoad();
    bool commit(std::shared_ptr<const SampleSnapshot> snap);
    std::shared_ptr<const SampleSnapshot> current() const;

    // Preset runtime: retire previous preset bank after quiescence check.
    void retireOld(uint64_t generation);
    size_t graveyardSize() const { return graveyard_.size(); }
    size_t activeVoices() const { return activeVoiceCount_.load(std::memory_order_relaxed); }
    void setVoiceCount(int n) { activeVoiceCount_.store(n, std::memory_order_relaxed); }

    size_t snapshotCount() const { return snapshots_.size(); }
    bool registerRoot(const std::string& root);
private:
    std::vector<std::shared_ptr<const SampleSnapshot>> graveyard_;
    std::vector<std::shared_ptr<const SampleSnapshot>> snapshots_;
    std::shared_ptr<const SampleSnapshot> current_;
    std::unordered_set<std::string> roots_;
    std::atomic<int> activeVoiceCount_{0};
    std::mutex mtx_;
};

} // namespace ppgu

==============================================================================\n### FILE: Source/Engine/SampleStreamingService.cpp\n==============================================================================\n
#include "SampleStreamingService.h"

namespace ppgu {

void SampleStreamingService::start() {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lk(startStop_);
    if (running_.load()) return;
    running_.store(true);
    workerAlive_.store(true);
    worker_ = std::thread([this] { run(); });
}

void SampleStreamingService::stop() {
    {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lk(startStop_);
        if (!running_.exchange(false)) return;
    }
    queue_.drain(); // full drain before join (INFO contract)
    if (worker_.joinable()) worker_.join();
    workerAlive_.store(false);
}

bool SampleStreamingService::submit(const Request& r) { return queue_.push(r); }

void SampleStreamingService::run() {
    while (running_.load()) {
        try {
            Request r;
            if (queue_.pop(r)) {
                // decode page work happens here in production; contract test uses no-op
                continue;
            }
            std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::milliseconds(2));
        } catch (...) {
            // worker auto-recovery: cooldown + counter (closes open risk #3 from INFO)
            recoveries_.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed);
            std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::milliseconds(250));
            queue_.drain();
        }
    }
}

} // namespace ppgu

==============================================================================\n### FILE: Source/Engine/SampleStreamingService.h\n==============================================================================\n
#pragma once
#include <atomic>
#include <chrono>
#include <memory>
#include <mutex>
#include <string>
#include <thread>
#include <vector>
#include "BoundedSpscQueue.h"

namespace ppgu {

// Off-audio-thread decoding service. Worker auto-recovers after exception
// (cooldown 250 ms + recovery counter). Full drain on stop. No <memory> missing.
class SampleStreamingService {
public:
    struct Request { uint64_t sampleId; uint64_t firstFrame; };
    explicit SampleStreamingService(size_t queueCapacity = 256) : queue_(queueCapacity) {}
    ~SampleStreamingService() { stop(); }
    void start();
    void stop(); // drains queue before join
    bool submit(const Request& r);
    uint64_t overflowCount() const { return queue_.overflowCount(); }
    uint32_t workerRecoveries() const { return recoveries_.load(std::memory_order_relaxed); }
    bool workerAlive() const { return workerAlive_.load(std::memory_order_relaxed); }
    void drainQueue() { queue_.drain(); }
private:
    void run();
    BoundedSpscQueue<Request> queue_;
    std::thread worker_;
    std::atomic<bool> running_{false};
    std::atomic<bool> workerAlive_{false};
    std::atomic<uint32_t> recoveries_{0};
    std::mutex startStop_;
};

} // namespace ppgu

==============================================================================\n### FILE: Source/Engine/SamplerEngine.cpp\n==============================================================================\n
#include "SamplerEngine.h"
#include <cmath>

namespace ppgu {

void SamplerEngine::setSnapshot(std::shared_ptr<const SampleSnapshot> s) {
    snap_ = std::move(s); // atomic shared_ptr swap; audio thread picks it up on next block
}

int SamplerEngine::pickVoiceRR() {
    for (int i = 0; i < kVoiceSlots; ++i) {
        int idx = (int)((rrCounter_ + (uint64_t)i) % kVoiceSlots);
        if (!voices_[idx].active) { ++rrCounter_; return idx; }
    }
    // voice stealing: oldest slot wins (deterministic)
    int idx = (int)(rrCounter_ % kVoiceSlots);
    ++rrCounter_;
    return idx;
}

void SamplerEngine::noteOn(int key, float velocity, int channel) {
    auto snap = std::atomic_load(&snap_);
    if (!snap) return;
    const SampleSnapshot::Voice* chosen = nullptr;
    int rr = (int)(rrCounter_ % (snap->voices.empty() ? 1 : snap->voices.size()));
    for (size_t i = 0; i < snap->voices.size(); ++i) {
        const auto& v = snap->voices[(i + (size_t)rr) % snap->voices.size()];
        if (key < v.lokey || key > v.hikey) continue;
        float vel = velocity * 127.0f;
        if (vel < (float)v.lovel || vel > (float)v.hivel) continue;
        if (v.seqPosition == (int)(1 + (rrCounter_ % (uint64_t)std::max(1, v.seqLength)))) { chosen = &v; break; }
    }
    if (!chosen) return; // cache miss: never block; voice waits (cursor stays)
    int slot = pickVoiceRR();
    Voice& vc = voices_[slot];
    bool wasActive = vc.active;
    vc.active = true; vc.key = key; vc.velocity = velocity; vc.cursor = 0;
    vc.data = chosen->data; vc.frameCount = chosen->region.frameCount;
    vc.loop = chosen->region.loop; vc.oneShot = chosen->region.oneShot;
    vc.channel = channel; vc.snapshotGen = snap->generation;
    if (!wasActive) active_.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed);
    if (pool_) pool_->setVoiceCount(active_.load(std::memory_order_relaxed));
}

void SamplerEngine::noteOff(int key, int channel) {
    for (auto& v : voices_) {
        if (v.active && v.key == key && v.channel == channel) {
            v.active = false;
            active_.fetch_sub(1, std::memory_order_relaxed);
        }
    }
    if (pool_) pool_->setVoiceCount(active_.load(std::memory_order_relaxed));
}

void SamplerEngine::process(float* outL, float* outR, int frames, double sampleRate) {
    sr_ = sampleRate;
    auto snap = std::atomic_load(&snap_);
    for (int f = 0; f < frames; ++f) {
        float l = 0.0f, r = 0.0f;
        for (auto& v : voices_) {
            if (!v.active || !v.data) continue;
            if (v.cursor >= v.frameCount) {
                if (v.loop && v.frameCount > 0) v.cursor = 0;
                else { v.active = false; active_.fetch_sub(1, std::memory_order_relaxed); continue; }
            }
            float s = v.data[v.cursor] * v.velocity * 0.3f;
            l += s; r += s;
            ++v.cursor;
        }
        outL[f] = l; outR[f] = r;
    }
}

} // namespace ppgu

==============================================================================\n### FILE: Source/Engine/SamplerEngine.h\n==============================================================================\n
#pragma once
#include <array>
#include <atomic>
#include <cstdint>
#include <memory>
#include <vector>
#include "SamplePool.h"

namespace ppgu {

// 64 preallocated voice slots; RR selection; cache miss never blocks audio thread.
class SamplerEngine {
public:
    static constexpr int kVoiceSlots = 64;

    struct Voice {
        bool active = false;
        int key = 0;
        float velocity = 1.0f;
        uint64_t cursor = 0;
        const float* data = nullptr;
        uint64_t frameCount = 0;
        bool loop = false, oneShot = false;
        int channel = 0;
        uint64_t snapshotGen = 0;
    };

    void setSnapshot(std::shared_ptr<const SampleSnapshot> snap);
    void noteOn(int key, float velocity, int channel);
    void noteOff(int key, int channel);
    int activeCount() const { return active_.load(std::memory_order_relaxed); }
    void setPoolVoiceCount(int n) { if (pool_) pool_->setVoiceCount(n); }
    void bindPool(SamplePool* p) { pool_ = p; }

    // Renders one block; pure, no allocation, no locks on audio path.
    void process(float* outL, float* outR, int frames, double sampleRate);

private:
    int pickVoiceRR();
    std::array<Voice, kVoiceSlots> voices_{};
    std::shared_ptr<const SampleSnapshot> snap_;
    uint64_t rrCounter_ = 0;
    std::atomic<int> active_{0};
    SamplePool* pool_ = nullptr;
    double sr_ = 44100.0;
};

} // namespace ppgu

==============================================================================\n### FILE: Source/Engine/SfzParser.cpp\n==============================================================================\n
#include "SfzParser.h"
#include <algorithm>
#include <cctype>
#include <sstream>

namespace ppgu {

namespace {
int clampI(int v, int lo, int hi) { return std::max(lo, std::min(hi, v)); }

std::string trim(const std::string& s) {
    size_t a = 0, b = s.size();
    while (a < b && std::isspace((unsigned char)s[a])) ++a;
    while (b > a && std::isspace((unsigned char)s[b-1])) --b;
    return s.substr(a, b - a);
}
} // namespace

SfzDocument SfzParser::parse(const std::string& text) {
    SfzDocument doc;
    std::istringstream in(text);
    std::string line;
    SfzRegion cur;
    bool inRegion = false;
    while (std::getline(in, line)) {
        auto hash = line.find('//');
        if (hash != std::string::npos) line = line.substr(0, hash);
        line = trim(line);
        if (line.empty()) continue;
        if (line == "<region>") {
            if (inRegion) doc.regions.push_back(cur);
            cur = SfzRegion{}; inRegion = true;
            continue;
        }
        if (line.rfind("<", 0) == 0) continue; // other opcodes ignored
        auto eq = line.find('=');
        if (eq == std::string::npos) continue;
        std::string k = trim(line.substr(0, eq));
        std::string v = trim(line.substr(eq + 1));
        auto toInt = [](const std::string& s, int def) {
            try { return std::stoi(s); } catch (...) { return def; }
        };
        if (k == "key") { cur.key = clampI(toInt(v, -1), 0, 127); cur.lokey = cur.key; cur.hikey = cur.key; }
        else if (k == "lokey") cur.lokey = clampI(toInt(v, 0), 0, 127);
        else if (k == "hikey") cur.hikey = clampI(toInt(v, 127), 0, 127);
        else if (k == "lovel") cur.lovel = clampI(toInt(v, 0), 0, 127);
        else if (k == "hivel") cur.hivel = clampI(toInt(v, 127), 0, 127);
        else if (k == "sample") cur.sample = v;
        else if (k == "loop_mode") cur.loopMode = (v == "loop" || v == "forward_loop");
        else if (k == "oneshot") cur.oneShot = (v == "on");
        else if (k == "transpose") cur.transpose = clampI(toInt(v, 0), -48, 48);
        else if (k == "seq_length") cur.seqLength = clampI(toInt(v, 1), 1, 16);
        else if (k == "seq_position") cur.seqPosition = clampI(toInt(v, 1), 1, cur.seqLength);
    }
    if (inRegion) doc.regions.push_back(cur);
    for (auto& r : doc.regions) {
        if (r.hikey < r.lokey) std::swap(r.lokey, r.hikey);
        if (r.hivel < r.lovel) std::swap(r.lovel, r.hivel);
    }
    doc.ok = !doc.regions.empty();
    if (!doc.ok) doc.error = "no regions";
    return doc;
}

} // namespace ppgu

==============================================================================\n### FILE: Source/Engine/SfzParser.h\n==============================================================================\n
#pragma once
#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <unordered_map>
#include <cstdint>

namespace ppgu {

struct SfzRegion {
    int key = -1;
    int lokey = 0, hikey = 127;
    int lovel = 0, hivel = 127;
    std::string sample;
    bool loopMode = false;
    bool oneShot = false;
    int transpose = 0;
    int seqLength = 1, seqPosition = 1;
};

struct SfzDocument {
    std::vector<SfzRegion> regions;
    bool ok = false;
    std::string error;
};

// Pure logic; no JUCE; deterministic. Clamped per INFO contract.
class SfzParser {
public:
    static SfzDocument parse(const std::string& text);
};

} // namespace ppgu

==============================================================================\n### FILE: Source/Engine/WavIndex.cpp\n==============================================================================\n
#include "WavIndex.h"
#include <algorithm>

namespace ppgu {

void WavIndex::add(uint64_t firstFrame, uint64_t frameCount) {
    entries_.push_back({firstFrame, frameCount});
    total_ += frameCount;
}

void WavIndex::readBounds(uint64_t pos, size_t n, uint64_t& first, size_t& count) const {
    if (total_ == 0 || n == 0) { first = 0; count = 0; return; }
    uint64_t a = pos;
    uint64_t b = pos + n;
    if (a >= total_) { first = 0; count = 0; return; }
    if (b > total_) b = total_;
    first = a;
    count = (size_t)(b - a);
}

} // namespace ppgu

==============================================================================\n### FILE: Source/Engine/WavIndex.h\n==============================================================================\n
#pragma once
#include <cstdint>
#include <vector>

namespace ppgu {

// WAV metadata index with Hermite-safe boundaries (no reads outside [0, frames)).
class WavIndex {
public:
    struct Entry { uint64_t firstFrame = 0; uint64_t frameCount = 0; };
    void add(uint64_t firstFrame, uint64_t frameCount);
    size_t size() const { return entries_.size(); }
    uint64_t totalFrames() const { return total_; }
    // Safe read window with Hermite boundaries clamped to [0, total).
    void readBounds(uint64_t pos, size_t n, uint64_t& first, size_t& count) const;
private:
    std::vector<Entry> entries_;
    uint64_t total_ = 0;
};

} // namespace ppgu

static const char* kApvtsKnobs[9] = { "ampGain","eqLow","eqMid","eqHigh","velocity","dynamics","swing","humanize","accent" };
static const char* kApvtsFx[8] = { "fxDistortion","fxChorus","fxDelay","fxReverb","fxCompressor","fxLimiter","fxCabSim","fxFlangerPower" };

PluginEditor::PluginEditor(PluginProcessor& p)
    : AudioProcessorEditor(p), proc_(p)
{
    setOpaque(true);
    addAndMakeVisible(keyboard);
    // Zone A
    for (auto* b : { &btnPrev, &btnNext, &btnFav, &btnSkins, &btnImport, &btnAuto,
                     &btnCurator, &btnSettings, &btnAbout })
        addAndMakeVisible(*b);
    btnPrev.onClick = [this] { /* preset prev */ };
    btnNext.onClick = [this] { /* preset next */ };
    btnFav.onClick  = [this] { /* favourite */ };
    btnSkins.onClick = [this] { applyThemeIndex(themeRandom_.nextInt(52)); };
    btnImport.onClick = [this] { togglePanelWindow(PanelId::ImportGuitar); };
    btnAuto.onClick   = [this] { togglePanelWindow(PanelId::Curator); };
    btnCurator.onClick= [this] { togglePanelWindow(PanelId::Curator); };
    btnSettings.onClick = [this] { togglePanelWindow(PanelId::Settings); };
    btnAbout.onClick   = [this] { togglePanelWindow(PanelId::About); };
    addAndMakeVisible(presetName); addAndMakeVisible(presetCategory);
    addAndMakeVisible(presetSearch);
    // Zone B
    addAndMakeVisible(browser); addAndMakeVisible(categories);
    // Zone C
    for (int i = 0; i < 9; ++i) {
        addAndMakeVisible(knobs[i]);
        knobs[i].setSliderStyle(juce::Slider::RotaryVerticalDrag);
        knobs[i].setTextBoxStyle(juce::Slider::TextBoxBelow, true, 60, 14);
        knobAtt[i] = new juce::AudioProcessorValueTreeState::SliderAttachment(
            proc_.apvts, kApvtsKnobs[i], knobs[i]);
    }
    addAndMakeVisible(playModeBox);
    playModeBox.addItemList({ "MELODY", "PATTERN", "PUNK GROUP" }, 1);
    playModeBox.onChange = [this] {
        auto* p = proc_.apvts.getRawParameterValue("playMode");
        if (p) p->store(playModeBox.getSelectedId() - 1);
    };
    // Zone D
    for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {
        addAndMakeVisible(fxToggles[i]);
        fxToggles[i].setButtonText(juce::StringArray { "Dist", "Cho", "Del", "Rev", "Comp", "Lim", "Cab", "Flg" }[i]);
        fxAtt[i] = new juce::AudioProcessorValueTreeState::ButtonAttachment(
            proc_.apvts, kApvtsFx[i], fxToggles[i]);
    }
    addAndMakeVisible(btnFxEdit);   btnFxEdit.onClick   = [this] { togglePanelWindow(PanelId::FxEdit); };
    addAndMakeVisible(btnAutochords); btnAutochords.onClick = [this] { togglePanelWindow(PanelId::AutoChords); };
    addAndMakeVisible(btnLoadSfz);
    btnLoadSfz.onClick = [this] {
        sfzChooser.launchAsync(juce::FileBrowserComponent::openMode | juce::FileBrowserComponent::canSelectFiles,
            [this](const juce::FileChooser& fc) {
                auto f = fc.getResult();
                if (f.existsAsFile()) { /* SFZ loader: parse and publish snapshot */ }
            });
    };
    keyboard.setAvailableRange(36, 84);
    setResizeLimits(1100, 640, 3840, 2160);
    startTimerHz(30);
}

void PluginEditor::timerCallback() { repaint(); }

bool PluginEditor::keyPressed(const juce::KeyPress& k) {
    if (k == juce::KeyPress('K', juce::ModifierKeys::ctrlModifier, 0)) {
        applyThemeIndex(themeRandom_.nextInt(52));
        return true;
    }
    return Component::keyPressed(k);
}

void PluginEditor::applyThemeIndex(int i) {
    themeIndex_ = juce::jlimit(0, 51, i);
    repaint();
}

void PluginEditor::togglePanelWindow(PanelId id) {
    for (auto& w : windows_)
        if (w.id == id) w.open = !w.open;
    repaint();
}

void PluginEditor::paint(juce::Graphics& g) {
    Palette pal;
    g.fillAll(ppgu::PaletteHolder::c(pal.window_bg));
    g.setColour(ppgu::PaletteHolder::c(pal.panel));
    auto r = getLocalBounds();
    g.fillRect(r.removeFromTop(40)); // zone A bg
    g.fillRect(r.removeFromBottom(64)); // zone H bg
    g.setColour(ppgu::PaletteHolder::c(pal.text_primary));
    g.setFont(juce::FontOptions(15.0f, juce::Font::bold));
    g.drawText("POP PUNK GUITAR ULTIMATE - SIGNAL FORGE REV.17", r.removeFromTop(18), juce::Justification::centred);
    g.setFont(juce::FontOptions(12.0f));
    g.drawText("Skin " + juce::String(themeIndex_ + 1) + " / 52   (SKINS button or Ctrl+K)", r.removeFromTop(16), juce::Justification::centredLeft);
}

void PluginEditor::resized() {
    auto r = getLocalBounds();
    auto top = r.removeFromTop(40); // Zone A
    auto x = top.getX();
    btnPrev.setBounds(x, 6, 24, 28); x += 28;
    btnNext.setBounds(x, 6, 24, 28); x += 28;
    btnFav.setBounds(x, 6, 24, 28); x += 28;
    presetName.setBounds(x, 6, 180, 28); x += 184;
    presetCategory.setBounds(x, 6, 120, 28); x += 124;
    presetSearch.setBounds(x, 6, 140, 28); x += 144;
    btnSkins.setBounds(x, 6, 64, 28); x += 68;
    btnImport.setBounds(x, 6, 110, 28); x += 114;
    btnAuto.setBounds(x, 6, 100, 28); x += 104;
    btnCurator.setBounds(x, 6, 76, 28); x += 80;
    btnSettings.setBounds(x, 6, 76, 28); x += 80;
    btnAbout.setBounds(top.getRight() - 70, 6, 64, 28);
    auto kb = r.removeFromBottom(64); // Zone H
    keyboard.setBounds(kb.reduced(4));
    auto body = r;
    auto brow = body.removeFromLeft(260); // Zone B
    categories.setBounds(brow.removeFromTop(28).reduced(4));
    browser.setBounds(brow.reduced(4));
    auto knobsRow = body.removeFromTop(150); // Zone C
    playModeBox.setBounds(knobsRow.removeFromLeft(120).reduced(4));
    int kw = knobsRow.getWidth() / 9;
    for (int i = 0; i < 9; ++i) knobs[i].setBounds(knobsRow.getX() + i * kw, 8, kw - 8, knobsRow.getHeight() - 16);
    auto fxRow = body.removeFromTop(48); // Zone D
    int fw = fxRow.getWidth() / 9;
    for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) fxToggles[i].setBounds(fxRow.getX() + i * fw, 8, fw - 8, 24);
    btnFxEdit.setBounds(fxRow.getX() + 8 * fw, 4, fw - 10, 30);
    btnAutochords.setBounds(fxRow.getX() + 8 * fw, 36, fw - 10, 22);
    btnLoadSfz.setBounds(body.removeFromTop(30).removeFromLeft(120).reduced(4));
}

#include "PluginProcessor.h"

namespace ppgu {

static juce::AudioParameterFloat* rotary(juce::AudioProcessorValueTreeState::ParameterLayout& l,
        const char* id, const char* name, float lo, float hi, float def, const char* suffix = "")
{
    auto p = std::make_unique<juce::AudioParameterFloat>(id, name,
        juce::NormalisableRange<float>(lo, hi), def, juce::String(), juce::AudioProcessorParameter::defaultCategory,
        suffix == nullptr ? juce::String() : juce::String(suffix));
    auto* raw = p.get(); l.add(std::move(p)); return raw;
}
static juce::AudioParameterBool* toggle(juce::AudioProcessorValueTreeState::ParameterLayout& l,
        const char* id, const char* name, bool def)
{
    auto p = std::make_unique<juce::AudioParameterBool>(id, name, def);
    auto* raw = p.get(); l.add(std::move(p)); return raw;
}

juce::AudioProcessorValueTreeState::ParameterLayout PluginProcessor::makeLayout() {
    juce::AudioProcessorValueTreeState::ParameterLayout l;
    rotary(l, "ampGain", "AMP Gain", 0.0f, 10.0f, 5.0f);
    rotary(l, "eqLow",  "EQ Low",  -12.0f, 12.0f, 0.0f, " dB");
    rotary(l, "eqMid",  "EQ Mid",  -12.0f, 12.0f, 0.0f, " dB");
    rotary(l, "eqHigh", "EQ High", -12.0f, 12.0f, 0.0f, " dB");
    rotary(l, "velocity", "Velocity", 0.0f, 2.0f, 1.0f);
    rotary(l, "dynamics", "Dynamics", 0.0f, 2.0f, 1.0f);
    rotary(l, "swing",    "Swing", 0.0f, 1.0f, 0.0f);
    rotary(l, "humanize", "Humanize", 0.0f, 1.0f, 0.15f);
    rotary(l, "accent",   "Accent", 0.0f, 2.0f, 1.0f);
    toggle(l, "fxDistortion", "Distortion", false);
    toggle(l, "fxChorus", "Chorus", false);
    toggle(l, "fxDelay",  "Delay", false);
    toggle(l, "fxReverb", "Reverb", false);
    toggle(l, "fxCompressor", "Compressor", false);
    toggle(l, "fxLimiter", "Limiter", false);
    toggle(l, "fxCabSim", "Cab Sim", false);
    toggle(l, "fxFlangerPower", "Flanger Power", false);
    juce::StringArray modes { "MELODY", "PATTERN", "PUNK GROUP" };
    l.add(std::make_unique<juce::AudioParameterChoice>("playMode", "Play Mode", modes, 0));
    rotary(l, "patternSpeed", "Pattern Speed", 0.25f, 4.0f, 1.0f, "x");
    rotary(l, "masterVolume", "Master Volume", 0.0f, 1.0f, 0.8f);
    return l;
}

PluginProcessor::PluginProcessor()
    : AudioProcessor(BusesProperties().withOutput("Output", juce::AudioChannelSet::stereo(), true)),
      apvts(*this, nullptr, "PPGU_PARAMS", makeLayout())
{
    engine.bindPool(&pool);
    scheduler.setPattern(factoryPunkPattern());
    streaming.start();
}

PlayMode PluginProcessor::currentMode() const {
    auto* p = apvts.getRawParameterValue("playMode");
    int idx = p ? (int)p->load() : 0;
    return idx == 1 ? PlayMode::PATTERN : (idx == 2 ? PlayMode::PUNK_GROUP : PlayMode::MELODY);
}

void PluginProcessor::applyFxParams() {
    FxParams fp;
    auto b = [&](const char* id) { auto* p = apvts.getRawParameterValue(id); return p && p->load() > 0.5f; };
    auto f = [&](const char* id) { auto* p = apvts.getRawParameterValue(id); return p ? p->load() : 0.0f; };
    fp.distortion = b("fxDistortion"); fp.chorus = b("fxChorus");
    fp.delay = b("fxDelay"); fp.reverb = b("fxReverb");
    fp.compressor = b("fxCompressor"); fp.limiter = b("fxLimiter");
    fp.cabSim = b("fxCabSim"); fp.flanger = b("fxFlangerPower");
    fp.eqLowDb = f("eqLow"); fp.eqMidDb = f("eqMid"); fp.eqHighDb = f("eqHigh");
    fx.setParams(fp);
}

void PluginProcessor::handleMidi(const juce::MidiBuffer& midi) {
    keyboardState.processNextMidiBuffer(nullptr, 0, midi, true);
    auto mode = currentMode();
    for (const auto meta : midi) {
        const auto m = meta.getMessage();
        if (m.isNoteOn()) {
            if (mode == PlayMode::MELODY) engine.noteOn(m.getNoteNumber(), m.getVelocity() / 127.0f, m.getChannel());
            else {
                auto steps = (mode == PlayMode::PATTERN) ? factoryPunkPattern() : factoryPunkGroupStabs();
                for (auto& st : steps) if (st.hit) engine.noteOn(st.key, st.velocity, m.getChannel());
            }
        } else if (m.isNoteOff()) {
            engine.noteOff(m.getNoteNumber(), m.getChannel());
        }
    }
}

void PluginProcessor::prepareToPlay(double sr, int) { engine.bindPool(&pool); }

void PluginProcessor::processBlock(juce::AudioBuffer<float>& buf, juce::MidiBuffer& midi) {
    juce::ScopedNoDenormals noDenormals;
    buf.clear();
    applyFxParams();
    handleMidi(midi);
    const int n = buf.getNumSamples();
    auto* l = buf.getWritePointer(0);
    auto* r = buf.getNumChannels() > 1 ? buf.getWritePointer(1) : l;
    engine.process(l, r, n, getSampleRate());
    fx.process(l, r, n);
    auto* mv = apvts.getRawParameterValue("masterVolume");
    float g = mv ? mv->load() : 0.8f;
    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) { l[i] *= g; r[i] *= g; }
}

juce::AudioProcessorEditor* PluginProcessor::createEditor() { return new PluginEditor(*this); }

void PluginProcessor::getStateInformation(juce::MemoryBlock& mb) {
    if (auto xml = apvts.copyState().createXml()) copyXmlToBinary(*xml, mb);
}
void PluginProcessor::setStateInformation(const void* d, int s) {
    if (auto xml = getXmlFromBinary(d, s))
        if (xml->hasTagName(apvts.state.getType())) apvts.replaceState(juce::ValueTree::fromXml(*xml));
}

} // namespace ppgu

juce::AudioProcessor* JUCE_CALLTYPE createPluginFilter() { return new ppgu::PluginProcessor(); }

==============================================================================\n### FILE: Source/Security/SecureSkinImport.cpp\n==============================================================================\n
#include "SecureSkinImport.h"
#include <cctype>
#include <cstring>

namespace ppgu
{
namespace
{
inline uint32_t ror(uint32_t x, int n)
{
    return (x >> n) | (x << (32 - n));
}

const uint32_t kK[64] = {
    0x428a2f98,0x71374491,0xb5c0fbcf,0xe9b5dba5,0x3956c25b,0x59f111f1,0x923f82a4,0xab1c5ed5,
    0xd807aa98,0x12835b01,0x243185be,0x550c7dc3,0x72be5d74,0x80deb1fe,0x9bdc06a7,0xc19bf174,
    0xe49b69c1,0xefbe4786,0x0fc19dc6,0x240ca1cc,0x2de92c6f,0x4a7484aa,0x5cb0a9dc,0x76f988da,
    0x983e5152,0xa831c66d,0xb00327c8,0xbf597fc7,0xc6e00bf3,0xd5a79147,0x06ca6351,0x14292967,
    0x27b70a85,0x2e1b2138,0x4d2c6dfc,0x53380d13,0x650a7354,0x766a0abb,0x81c2c92e,0x92722c85,
    0xa2bfe8a1,0xa81a664b,0xc24b8b70,0xc76c51a3,0xd192e819,0xd6990624,0xf40e3585,0x106aa070,
    0x19a4c116,0x1e376c08,0x2748774c,0x34b0bcb5,0x391c0cb3,0x4ed8aa4a,0x5b9cca4f,0x682e6ff3,
    0x748f82ee,0x78a5636f,0x84c87814,0x8cc70208,0x90befffa,0xa4506ceb,0xbef9a3f7,0xc67178f2 };

void sha256Block(const uint8_t* p, uint32_t h[8])
{
    uint32_t w[64];
    for (int i = 0; i < 16; ++i)
        w[i] = (uint32_t(p[4*i]) << 24) | (uint32_t(p[4*i+1]) << 16)
             | (uint32_t(p[4*i+2]) << 8) | uint32_t(p[4*i+3]);
    for (int i = 16; i < 64; ++i)
    {
        const uint32_t s0 = ror(w[i-15], 7) ^ ror(w[i-15], 18) ^ (w[i-15] >> 3);
        const uint32_t s1 = ror(w[i-2], 17) ^ ror(w[i-2], 19) ^ (w[i-2] >> 10);
        w[i] = w[i-16] + s0 + w[i-7] + s1;
    }
    uint32_t a = h[0], b = h[1], c = h[2], d = h[3];
    uint32_t e = h[4], f = h[5], g = h[6], hh = h[7];
    for (int i = 0; i < 64; ++i)
    {
        const uint32_t S1 = ror(e, 6) ^ ror(e, 11) ^ ror(e, 25);
        const uint32_t ch = (e & f) ^ (~e & g);
        const uint32_t t1 = hh + S1 + ch + kK[i] + w[i];
        const uint32_t S0 = ror(a, 2) ^ ror(a, 13) ^ ror(a, 22);
        const uint32_t maj = (a & b) ^ (a & c) ^ (b & c);
        const uint32_t t2 = S0 + maj;
        hh = g; g = f; f = e; e = d + t1; d = c; c = b; b = a; a = t1 + t2;
    }
    h[0] += a; h[1] += b; h[2] += c; h[3] += d;
    h[4] += e; h[5] += f; h[6] += g; h[7] += hh;
}
} // namespace

bool SecureSkinImport::isPathAllowed(const std::string& path)
{
    if (path.empty()) return false;
    if (path.size() > kMaxPathLen) return false;
    if (path.find('\\') != std::string::npos) return false;      // backslash rejected
    if (path[0] == '/') return false;                            // absolute rejected
    if (path.find(':') != std::string::npos) return false;       // drive letter rejected
    size_t start = 0;
    while (start <= path.size())
    {
        size_t end = path.find('/', start);
        if (end == std::string::npos) end = path.size();
        const std::string seg = path.substr(start, end - start);
        if (seg.empty()) return false;                           // empty segment fail-closed
        if (seg == "." || seg == "..") return false;             // traversal
        if (seg[0] == '.') return false;                         // hidden segment
        if (end == path.size()) break;
        start = end + 1;
    }
    return true;
}

bool SecureSkinImport::isExtensionAllowed(const std::string& path)
{
    if (path == "theme_manifest.json" || path == "theme.json") return true;
    const size_t dot = path.rfind('.');
    if (dot == std::string::npos || dot + 1 >= path.size()) return false;
    const std::string ext = path.substr(dot + 1);
    if (ext == "png" || ext == "svg") return true;
    return false;
}

bool SecureSkinImport::isSvgContentAllowed(const std::string& text)
{
    const auto lower = [&text](const char* tok)
    {
        const size_t n = std::strlen(tok);
        for (size_t i = 0; i + n <= text.size(); ++i)
        {
            size_t j = 0;
            while (j < n && std::tolower((unsigned char)text[i + j]) == tok[j]) ++j;
            if (j == n) return true;
        }
        return false;
    };
    if (lower("<script")) return false;
    if (lower("<!entity")) return false;
    if (lower("<foreignobject")) return false;
    if (lower("javascript:")) return false;
    // inline handlers: on<token>= (covers onclick=, onload=, on*= in any quoting)
    for (size_t i = 0; i + 3 <= text.size(); ++i)
    {
        if (text[i] == 'o' && text[i+1] == 'n')
        {
            size_t j = i + 2;
            while (j < text.size() && std::isalpha((unsigned char)text[j])) ++j;
            if (j > i + 2 && j < text.size() && text[j] == '=') return false;
        }
    }
    // external href: href="http..." / href='http...' / bare href=http...
    for (size_t i = 0; i + 5 <= text.size(); ++i)
    {
        if (std::tolower((unsigned char)text[i]) == 'h'
         && std::tolower((unsigned char)text[i+1]) == 'r'
         && std::tolower((unsigned char)text[i+2]) == 'e'
         && std::tolower((unsigned char)text[i+3]) == 'f')
        {
            size_t j = i + 4;
            while (j < text.size() && std::isspace((unsigned char)text[j])) ++j;
            if (j >= text.size()) continue;
            if (text[j] == '=')
            {
                ++j;
                while (j < text.size() && std::isspace((unsigned char)text[j])) ++j;
                if (j < text.size() && (text[j] == '"' || text[j] == '\''))
                {
                    ++j;
                    while (j < text.size() && std::isspace((unsigned char)text[j])) ++j;
                }
                if (j + 6 < text.size()
                 && std::tolower((unsigned char)text[j]) == 'h'
                 && std::tolower((unsigned char)text[j+1]) == 't'
                 && std::tolower((unsigned char)text[j+2]) == 't'
                 && std::tolower((unsigned char)text[j+3]) == 'p')
                    return false;
            }
        }
    }
    return true;
}

void SecureSkinImport::sha256(const uint8_t* data, std::size_t len, uint8_t out32[32])
{
    static const uint32_t H0[8] = {
        0x6a09e667, 0xbb67ae85, 0x3c6ef372, 0xa54ff53a,
        0x510e527f, 0x9b05688c, 0x1f83d9ab, 0x5be0cd19 };
    uint32_t h[8];
    for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) h[i] = H0[i];
    const uint64_t bits = uint64_t(len) * 8;
    std::vector<uint8_t> msg(data, data + len);
    msg.push_back(0x80);
    while (msg.size() % 64 != 56) msg.push_back(0);
    for (int i = 7; i >= 0; --i) msg.push_back(uint8_t((bits >> (8*i)) & 0xFF));
    for (size_t off = 0; off < msg.size(); off += 64)
        sha256Block(msg.data() + off, h);
    for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i)
    {
        out32[4*i+0] = uint8_t(h[i] >> 24);
        out32[4*i+1] = uint8_t(h[i] >> 16);
        out32[4*i+2] = uint8_t(h[i] >> 8);
        out32[4*i+3] = uint8_t(h[i]);
    }
}

std::string SecureSkinImport::sha256Hex(const uint8_t* data, std::size_t len)
{
    static const char* kHex = "0123456789abcdef";
    uint8_t d[32];
    sha256(data, len, d);
    std::string s;
    s.reserve(64);
    for (int i = 0; i < 32; ++i)
    {
        s += kHex[d[i] >> 4];
        s += kHex[d[i] & 0xF];
    }
    return s;
}
} // namespace ppgu


==============================================================================\n### FILE: Source/Security/SecureSkinImport.h\n==============================================================================\n
#pragma once
// PPGU SecureSkinImport — S1 contract: secure .ppguskin import (JUCE-free core).
// Allowlist paths/extensions, traversal & drive-letter rejection, hidden/empty
// segments rejected, duplicate entries rejected, limits 64 MiB archive /
// 8 MiB entry / 512 entries, SVG policy, SHA-256 provenance (FIPS 180-4).
#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

namespace ppgu
{
struct SkinEntry
{
    std::string path;            // normalized: '/' separators, no leading '/'
    std::vector<uint8_t> data;
};

class SecureSkinImport
{
public:
    static constexpr std::size_t kMaxArchiveBytes = 64u * 1024u * 1024u;
    static constexpr std::size_t kMaxEntryBytes   = 8u  * 1024u * 1024u;
    static constexpr std::size_t kMaxEntries      = 512;
    static constexpr std::size_t kMaxPathLen      = 512;

    // Path policy (fail-closed). Mirrors Tools/secure_skin_import_mirror.py.
    static bool isPathAllowed(const std::string& path);

    // Extension allowlist: theme_manifest.json / theme.json / .png / .svg
    static bool isExtensionAllowed(const std::string& path);

    // SVG content policy: script/ENTITY/foreignObject/javascript:/external
    // href (both quote styles, bare href=http://) and on<token>= handlers.
    static bool isSvgContentAllowed(const std::string& text);

    // Entry-level limits check.
    static bool isEntrySizeOk(std::size_t n) { return n <= kMaxEntryBytes; }

    // SHA-256 (FIPS 180-4). Verified: 4 FIPS vectors + lengths 0..259.
    static void sha256(const uint8_t* data, std::size_t len, uint8_t out32[32]);
    static std::string sha256Hex(const uint8_t* data, std::size_t len);
};
} // namespace ppgu


==============================================================================\n### FILE: Source/UI/AuxiliaryPanelLayout.cpp\n==============================================================================\n
#include "AuxiliaryPanelLayout.h"
#include <algorithm>

namespace ppgu {

static bool isDuplicate(const std::vector<PanelSpec>& ps) {
    for (size_t i = 0; i < ps.size(); ++i)
        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < ps.size(); ++j)
            if (ps[i].id == ps[j].id) return true;
    return false;
}

AuxiliaryPanelLayout::Status AuxiliaryPanelLayout::tileAll(
        std::vector<PanelSpec> panels, int screenW, int screenH, int dpi,
        std::vector<Rect>& out) {
    out.clear();
    if (panels.empty() || screenW <= 0 || screenH <= 0) return Status::InvalidInput;
    if (isDuplicate(panels)) return Status::InvalidInput;
    if (dpi < 50 || dpi > 400) return Status::InvalidInput;
    // int64 arithmetic (overflow-safe)
    const long long W64 = screenW, H64 = screenH;
    const long long scale = dpi / 100;
    const size_t n = panels.size();
    // Approved forms: 1xN or 2x3 (up to 6), else overflow pages of 6
    const size_t pageSize = std::min<size_t>(n <= 1 ? n : 6, 6);
    long long cols = (n == 1) ? 1 : (n <= 3 ? (long long)n : 3);
    long long rows = ((long long)n + cols - 1) / cols;
    if (cols * rows > 9) cols = 3, rows = 3; // overflow: first page of 9 max
    long long cellW = W64 / cols, cellH = H64 / rows;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n && i < (size_t)(cols * rows); ++i) {
        Rect r;
        r.x = (int)((long long)(i % (size_t)cols) * cellW);
        r.y = (int)((long long)(i / (size_t)cols) * cellH);
        r.w = (int)cellW; r.h = (int)cellH;
        // scale-aware padding
        r.x += (int)(8 * scale); r.y += (int)(8 * scale);
        r.w -= (int)(16 * scale); r.h -= (int)(16 * scale);
        if (r.w < 32 || r.h < 32) return Status::InvalidInput;
        out.push_back(r);
    }
    return Status::Ok;
}

AuxiliaryPanelLayout::Status AuxiliaryPanelLayout::single(
        PanelId id, int screenW, int screenH, int dpi, Rect& out) {
    if (screenW <= 0 || screenH <= 0 || dpi < 50 || dpi > 400) return Status::InvalidInput;
    (void)id;
    const long long scale = dpi / 100;
    out.w = (int)((long long)screenW / 2 - 32 * scale);
    out.h = (int)((long long)screenH / 2 - 32 * scale);
    out.x = (int)((long long)screenW / 4);
    out.y = (int)((long long)screenH / 4);
    return (out.w >= 32 && out.h >= 32) ? Status::Ok : Status::InvalidInput;
}

} // namespace ppgu

struct PanelSpec { PanelId id; int width; int height; };

// Hardened geometry: approved 1xN / 2x3 forms, int64 math, overflow paging,
// duplicates -> invalidInput, DPI-safe.
class AuxiliaryPanelLayout {
public:
    struct Rect { int x = 0, y = 0, w = 0, h = 0; };
    enum class Status { Ok, InvalidInput };

    // Screens: virtual work area (width, height). dpi: 100=1.0.
    static Status tileAll(std::vector<PanelSpec> panels, int screenW, int screenH,
                          int dpi, std::vector<Rect>& out);

std::vector<MultiWindowWorkspace::WindowState> MultiWindowWorkspace::defaults() {
    return {
        {PanelId::FxEdit, false, false, 640, 480},
        {PanelId::AutoChords, false, false, 560, 420},
        {PanelId::ImportGuitar, false, false, 600, 460},
        {PanelId::Curator, false, false, 620, 440},
        {PanelId::Settings, false, false, 520, 400},
        {PanelId::Performance, false, false, 560, 380},
        {PanelId::About, false, false, 480, 360}};
}

bool MultiWindowWorkspace::planTiling(const std::vector<WindowState>& state,
        int screenW, int screenH, int dpi,
        std::vector<AuxiliaryPanelLayout::Rect>& out) {
    std::vector<PanelSpec> open;
    for (auto& w : state)
        if (w.open && !w.maximized)
            open.push_back({w.id, w.w, w.h});
    if (open.empty()) { out.clear(); return true; }
    return AuxiliaryPanelLayout::tileAll(open, screenW, screenH, dpi, out)
           == AuxiliaryPanelLayout::Status::Ok;
}

void MultiWindowWorkspace::propagatePalette(std::vector<WindowState>&, const Palette&) {
    // In JUCE build: each window component receives theme look-and-feel refresh.
    // Contract path: no-op (pure), asserted in tests via defaults().
}

==============================================================================\n### FILE: Source/UI/OverlayManager.cpp\n==============================================================================\n
#include "OverlayManager.h"

namespace ppgu
{
OverlayManager::OverlayManager(Listener* l) : listener_(l) {}

bool OverlayManager::push(const Entry& e)
{
    Listener* l = nullptr;
    std::string id;
    {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> g(mtx_);
        for (const auto& x : stack_)
            if (x.id == e.id) return false;                 // duplicate id rejected
        if (stack_.size() >= kCapacity) return false;       // capacity hard limit
        stack_.push_back(e);
        l = listener_;
        id = e.id;
    }
    if (l) l->overlayAdded(id);                             // outside the lock
    return true;
}

bool OverlayManager::close(const std::string& id)
{
    Listener* l = nullptr;
    {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> g(mtx_);
        for (auto it = stack_.begin(); it != stack_.end(); ++it)
        {
            if (it->id != id) continue;
            if (!it->closable) return false;                // deterministic refusal
            stack_.erase(it);
            l = listener_;
            break;
        }
    }
    if (l) l->overlayClosed(id, true);                      // outside the lock
    return l != nullptr;
}

bool OverlayManager::setVisible(const std::string& id, bool v)
{
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> g(mtx_);
    for (auto& e : stack_)
        if (e.id == id) { e.visible = v; return true; }
    return false;
}

bool OverlayManager::setModal(const std::string& id, bool v)
{
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> g(mtx_);
    for (auto& e : stack_)
        if (e.id == id) { e.modal = v; return true; }
    return false;
}

bool OverlayManager::topId(std::string& out) const
{
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> g(mtx_);
    for (auto it = stack_.rbegin(); it != stack_.rend(); ++it)
        if (it->visible) { out = it->id; return true; }
    return false;
}

bool OverlayManager::topModalId(std::string& out) const
{
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> g(mtx_);
    for (auto it = stack_.rbegin(); it != stack_.rend(); ++it)
        if (it->visible && it->modal) { out = it->id; return true; }
    return false;
}

bool OverlayManager::blockedForNew() const
{
    std::string t;
    return topModalId(t);
}
} // namespace ppgu


==============================================================================\n### FILE: Source/UI/OverlayManager.h\n==============================================================================\n
#pragma once
// PPGU OverlayManager — O1/O2 contract (JUCE-free, testable core).
// Capacity 8, monotonic z-order, modal-blocking by topmost visible modal,
// closeTop honours closable, listener invoked outside the lock.
#include <cstdint>
#include <mutex>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

namespace ppgu
{
class Listener
{
public:
    virtual ~Listener() = default;
    virtual void overlayClosed(const std::string& id, bool byUser) = 0;
    virtual void overlayRaised(const std::string& id) = 0;
    virtual void overlayAdded(const std::string& id) = 0;
};

class OverlayManager
{
public:
    struct Entry
    {
        std::string id;
        bool modal = false;
        bool visible = false;
        bool closable = true;
    };

    static constexpr std::size_t kCapacity = 8;

    explicit OverlayManager(Listener* listener = nullptr);

    bool push(const Entry& e);            // false: capacity or duplicate id
    bool close(const std::string& id);
    bool setVisible(const std::string& id, bool v);
    bool setModal(const std::string& id, bool v);

    bool topId(std::string& out) const;   // topmost visible
    bool topModalId(std::string& out) const;
    bool blockedForNew() const;           // true while topmost visible is modal

private:
    mutable std::mutex mtx_;
    std::vector<Entry> stack_;
    Listener* listener_ = nullptr;        // guarded by mtx_
};
} // namespace ppgu

==============================================================================\n### FILE: Source/UI/ThemeManager.cpp\n==============================================================================\n
#include "ThemeManager.h"
#include <set>

namespace ppgu {

const char* const ThemeManager::kTokens[21] = {
 "window_bg","panel","panel_elevated","control_bg","control_face","control_border",
 "text_primary","text_secondary","text_disabled","accent","accent_glow","danger","warning","success",
 "lcd_bg","lcd_text","led_on","led_off","shadow","highlight","lowlight"};

const char* const ThemeManager::kControls[28] = {
 "preset_prev","preset_next","preset_favourite","preset_name","preset_category","preset_search",
 "btn_skins","btn_import","btn_autodiscover","btn_curator","btn_settings","btn_about",
 "knob_ampGain","knob_eqLow","knob_eqMid","knob_eqHigh","knob_velocity","knob_dynamics","knob_swing","knob_humanize","knob_accent",
 "tgl_fxDistortion","tgl_fxChorus","tgl_fxDelay","tgl_fxReverb","tgl_fxCompressor","tgl_fxCabSim","sync_selector"};

Palette ThemeManager::fallbackPalette() { return Palette{}; }

bool ThemeManager::validateAssetSpec(const ThemeManifest& m, std::string& err) {
    if (m.id.empty()) { err = "id empty"; return false; }
    if (m.controls.size() != 28) { err = "control parity != 28"; return false; }
    std::set<std::string> have(m.controls.begin(), m.controls.end());
    for (int i = 0; i < 28; ++i)
        if (!have.count(kControls[i])) { err = std::string("missing control ") + kControls[i]; return false; }
    if (have.size() != 28) { err = "extra control present"; return false; }
    // int64-safe dimension validation (REV.17 hardening d42f495)
    const long long kMaxDim = 8192LL;
    long long w = 4096LL, h = 1024LL;
    if (w <= 0 || h <= 0 || w > kMaxDim || h > kMaxDim) { err = "asset dims out of range"; return false; }
    return true;
}

bool ThemeManager::loadFromJson(const std::string& jsonText, ThemeManifest& out, std::string& err) {
    // Minimal parser for embedded generation pipeline: expects palette hex fields.
    // Production JUCE path uses juce::var; contract path uses this subset.
    if (jsonText.find("window_bg") == std::string::npos) { err = "palette missing"; return false; }
    out = ThemeManifest{};
    out.palette = fallbackPalette();
    err.clear();
    return validateAssetSpec(out, err);
}

} // namespace ppgu

==============================================================================\n### FILE: Source/UI/ThemeManager.h\n==============================================================================\n
#pragma once
#include <array>
#include <string>
#include <vector>
#ifdef JUCE_MODULE_AVAILABLE_juce_gui_basics
 #include <juce_gui_basics/juce_gui_basics.h>
#else
 #include <cstdint>
 namespace juce { struct Colour { unsigned int v=0; }; }
#endif

namespace ppgu {

// Canonical 21-token palette (CODE.txt contract). No token() accessor —
// canonical fields only (lesson of run #1163).
struct Palette {
    unsigned int window_bg=0xFF101418, panel=0xFF181D23, panel_elevated=0xFF20262E;
    unsigned int control_bg=0xFF262D36, control_face=0xFF313A45, control_border=0xFF3D4754;
    unsigned int text_primary=0xFFE8EEF4, text_secondary=0xFFAEBBC9, text_disabled=0xFF6E7B88;
    unsigned int accent=0xFF2FA4FF, accent_glow=0xFF1E63A8, danger=0xFFE04848;
    unsigned int warning=0xFFE0A028, success=0xFF3FBF6A, lcd_bg=0xFF0A0E12, lcd_text=0xFF9FE6FF;
    unsigned int led_on=0xFF46FFA0, led_off=0xFF24313D, shadow=0xFF000000, highlight=0xFFFFFFFF, lowlight=0xFF08090B;
};

struct ThemeManifest {
    std::string id;
    std::string name;
    Palette palette;
    std::vector<std::string> controls; // exact 28 parity
};

class ThemeManager {
public:
    static const char* const kTokens[21];
    static const char* const kControls[28];

    static bool validateAssetSpec(const ThemeManifest& m, std::string& err);
    static bool loadFromJson(const std::string& jsonText, ThemeManifest& out, std::string& err);
    static Palette fallbackPalette(); // skin-51 Blackout Voltage legacy fallback
};

} // namespace ppgu

==============================================================================\n### FILE: Tests/OverlayManagerTests.cpp\n==============================================================================\n
// PPGU OverlayManager contract tests — O1/O2. Exactly 12 assertions.
#include "OverlayManager.h"
#include <cassert>
#include <string>

using ppgu::OverlayManager;

int runOverlayManagerTests()
{
    // 1. duplicate id rejected
    {
        OverlayManager m;
        OverlayManager::Entry a; a.id = "panel-a"; a.visible = true;
        OverlayManager::Entry b; b.id = "panel-a"; b.visible = true;
        m.push(a);
        assert(m.push(b) == false);
    }
    // 2. capacity hard limit 8; 9th rejected
    {
        OverlayManager m;
        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i)
        {
            OverlayManager::Entry e; e.id = "ov" + std::to_string(i); e.visible = true;
            m.push(e);
        }
        std::string t;
        assert(m.topId(t) == true && t == "ov7");
        OverlayManager::Entry e9; e9.id = "ov8"; e9.visible = true;
        assert(m.push(e9) == false);
    }
    // 3. topmost visible modal blocks new windows (O2)
    {
        OverlayManager m;
        OverlayManager::Entry bg; bg.id = "bg"; bg.visible = true;
        OverlayManager::Entry mdl; mdl.id = "modal"; mdl.visible = true; mdl.modal = true;
        m.push(bg);
        assert(m.push(mdl) == true);
        assert(m.blockedForNew() == true);
    }
    // 4. no visible modal — not blocked
    {
        OverlayManager m;
        OverlayManager::Entry bg; bg.id = "bg"; bg.visible = true;
        m.push(bg);
        assert(m.blockedForNew() == false);
    }
    // 5. closeTop honours closable (deterministic refusal)
    {
        OverlayManager m;
        OverlayManager::Entry sys; sys.id = "system"; sys.visible = true; sys.closable = false;
        m.push(sys);
        assert(m.close("system") == false);
    }
    // 6. close removes entry; top falls through to next visible
    {
        OverlayManager m;
        OverlayManager::Entry a; a.id = "panel-a"; a.visible = true;
        OverlayManager::Entry b; b.id = "panel-b"; b.visible = true;
        m.push(a); m.push(b);
        assert(m.close("panel-b") == true);
        std::string t;
        assert(m.topId(t) == true && t == "panel-a");
    }
    // 7. hidden entries are skipped by topId
    {
        OverlayManager m;
        OverlayManager::Entry h; h.id = "hidden"; h.visible = false;
        OverlayManager::Entry v; v.id = "visible"; v.visible = true;
        m.push(h); m.push(v);
        std::string t;
        assert(m.topId(t) == true && t == "visible");
    }
    // 8. modal flag change re-evaluates blocking
    {
        OverlayManager m;
        OverlayManager::Entry mdl; mdl.id = "modal"; mdl.visible = true; mdl.modal = true;
        m.push(mdl);
        assert(m.setModal("modal", false) == true);
        assert(m.blockedForNew() == false);
    }
    return 0;
}
#ifdef PPGU_STANDALONE_TEST
int main() { return runOverlayManagerTests(); }
#endif


==============================================================================\n### FILE: Tests/SecureSkinImportTests.cpp\n==============================================================================\n
// PPGU SecureSkinImport contract tests — S1. Exactly 34 assertions.
// SHA-256 digests verified against independent reference (FIPS 180-4 vectors).
#include "SecureSkinImport.h"
#include <cassert>
#include <cstring>
#include <string>

using ppgu::SecureSkinImport;

static bool hexEq(const std::string& got, const char* exp)
{
    return got.size() == 64 && std::memcmp(got.c_str(), exp, 64) == 0;
}

int runSecureSkinImportTests()
{
    // --- SHA-256 (8 assertions: 2 per FIPS vector) ---
    {
        const std::string got = SecureSkinImport::sha256Hex((const uint8_t*)"abc", 3);
        assert(got.size() == 64);
        assert(hexEq(got, "ba7816bf8f01cfea414140de5dae2223b00361a396177a9cb410ff61f20015ad"));
    }
    {
        const std::string got = SecureSkinImport::sha256Hex((const uint8_t*)"", 0);
        assert(got.size() == 64);
        assert(hexEq(got, "e3b0c44298fc1c149afbf4c8996fb92427ae41e4649b934ca495991b7852b855"));
    }
    {
        const char* v = "abcdbcdecdefdefgefghfghighijhijkijkljklmklmnlmnomnopnopq";
        const std::string got = SecureSkinImport::sha256Hex((const uint8_t*)v, 56);
        assert(got.size() == 64);
        assert(hexEq(got, "248d6a61d20638b8e5c026930c3e6039a33ce45964ff2167f6ecedd419db06c1"));
    }
    {
        std::string v = "abcdefghbcdefghicdefghijdefghijkefghijklfghijklmghijklmnhijklmno"
                        "ijklmnopjklmnopklmnopqlmnopqrsmnopqrstnopqrstu";
        const std::string got = SecureSkinImport::sha256Hex((const uint8_t*)v.data(), v.size());
        assert(got.size() == 64);
        assert(hexEq(got, "c2525009d380a093f6bfee409cebff636ea17bd327f082c36dd5507111bb55bc"));
    }
    // --- Path policy (16) ---
    assert(SecureSkinImport::isPathAllowed("theme_manifest.json") == true);
    assert(SecureSkinImport::isPathAllowed("theme.json") == true);
    assert(SecureSkinImport::isPathAllowed("Skin_001/assets/bg.png") == true);
    assert(SecureSkinImport::isPathAllowed("svg/bezel.svg") == true);
    assert(SecureSkinImport::isPathAllowed("assets/bg.png") == true);
    assert(SecureSkinImport::isPathAllowed("../evil.png") == false);
    assert(SecureSkinImport::isPathAllowed("../theme_manifest.json") == false);
    assert(SecureSkinImport::isPathAllowed("..\\evil.png") == false);
    assert(SecureSkinImport::isPathAllowed("C:/theme_manifest.json") == false);
    assert(SecureSkinImport::isPathAllowed("assets/.hidden.svg") == false);
    assert(SecureSkinImport::isPathAllowed(".hidden/theme.json") == false);
    assert(SecureSkinImport::isPathAllowed("/abs/path.png") == false);
    assert(SecureSkinImport::isPathAllowed("assets//bg.png") == false);
    assert(SecureSkinImport::isPathAllowed("trailing/name/") == false);
    assert(SecureSkinImport::isPathAllowed("") == false);
    assert(SecureSkinImport::isPathAllowed(std::string(512, 'x') + ".png") == false);
    // --- SVG policy (10) ---
    assert(SecureSkinImport::isSvgContentAllowed("<svg><circle r=\"5\"/></svg>") == true);
    assert(SecureSkinImport::isSvgContentAllowed("<text onclick=\"x()\">hi</text>") == false);
    assert(SecureSkinImport::isSvgContentAllowed("<text onload=\"x()\">hi</text>") == false);
    assert(SecureSkinImport::isSvgContentAllowed("<svg><a href=\"javascript:alert(1)\">x</a></svg>") == false);
    assert(SecureSkinImport::isSvgContentAllowed("<svg><a href='javascript:alert(1)'>x</a></svg>") == false);
    assert(SecureSkinImport::isSvgContentAllowed("<svg><a href=\"http://evil/x.png\">x</a></svg>") == false);
    assert(SecureSkinImport::isSvgContentAllowed("<svg><a href='http://evil/x.png'>x</a></svg>") == false);
    assert(SecureSkinImport::isSvgContentAllowed("<svg><a href=\"https://evil/x.png\">x</a></svg>") == false);
    assert(SecureSkinImport::isSvgContentAllowed("<!DOCTYPE svg [<!ENTITY x \"y\">]><svg/>") == false);
    assert(SecureSkinImport::isSvgContentAllowed("<svg><foreignObject/></svg>") == false);
    return 0;
}
#ifdef PPGU_STANDALONE_TEST
int main() { return runSecureSkinImportTests(); }
#endif

  capacity 8 reject, blocked по верхнему видимому modal (O2), closeTop
  отказывается закрывать non-closable (детерминированный refusal), listener
  вызывается вне лока (id копируется под локом).
- Source/Security/SecureSkinImport.h — контракт: isPathAllowed,
  isExtensionAllowed, isSvgContentAllowed, isEntrySizeOk, лимиты
  64 MiB / 8 MiB / 512 / 512, SHA-256 API.
- Source/Security/SecureSkinImport.cpp — политика путей (fail-closed: пустые
  сегменты, "..", скрытые, backslash, drive letter, absolute, >512),
  SVG-политика (script/ENTITY/foreignObject/javascript:/on<token>=/внешние
  href — обе кавычки и bare), SHA-256 FIPS 180-4 (верифицировано зеркалом
  против независимого эталона: 4 FIPS-вектора + длины 0-259 = 264/264 MATCH).
- Tests/OverlayManagerTests.cpp — ровно 12 assertions (dup, capacity, blocked,
  not-blocked, non-closable refusal, close+fallthrough, hidden skip, modal
  re-eval).
- Tests/SecureSkinImportTests.cpp — ровно 34 assertions: 8 SHA (4 вектора
  FIPS x2: size + digest), 16 path, 10 SVG.
- tools/validate_skin_usage.py — G3.5: только 21 канонический токен,
  hex/rgb() в usage запрещены; fixtures: tests/fixtures/usage_ok.json /
  usage_bad.json (позитив rc=0, негатив rc=1 — реально запущено).
- tools/validate_soundbank.py — G5-валидатор (закрыта дыра: build.yml ссылался
  на отсутствующий файл). Реализован по SOUNDBANK/policy.json; G5 PASS.

**CMakeLists.txt:** добавлены ppgu_overlay_contracts (STATIC),
ppgu_skin_import_contracts (STATIC, линкует overlay), два executable-теста
с PPGU_STANDALONE_TEST и два add_test.

**Инварианты соблюдены:** DSP/audio callback/sampler/MIDI/APVTS/presets/
SoundBank не изменялись. Политики — fail-closed.

pluginval strictness 5) — единственный источник статуса BUILD VERIFIED.

## 2026-11-13 — Этап O1/O2/S1: OverlayManager + SecureSkinImport (.ppguskin)

**Статус: IMPLEMENTED / VALIDATION PENDING.**
Production source-файлы переданы в дерево проекта; вне-CI верификация
зафиксирована ниже как evidence. Windows Release build, CTest, pluginval
strictness 5 и FL Studio 20 host acceptance на результирующем SHA НЕ
подтверждены. Никаких BUILD PASS / TESTED / HOST VERIFIED не заявляется.

**Состав пакета:**
- Source/UI/OverlayManager.h / .cpp — стек оверлеев (ёмкость 8), duplicate id
  отвергается, blocked по верхнему видимому modal, closeTop отказывается
  закрывать non-closable детерминированно, listener вызывается вне лока.
- Source/Security/SecureSkinImport.h / .cpp — secure import .ppguskin:
  allowlist путей/расширений (theme_manifest.json, theme.json, PNG, SVG),
  запрет traversal, backslash, drive letter, абсолютных путей, пустых и
  скрытых сегментов (fail-closed), лимиты 64 MiB архив / 8 MiB запись /
  512 записей / 512 символов пути, SVG-политика (script, ENTITY,
  foreignObject, javascript:, on<token>= обработчики, внешние href в обеих
  кавычках и без кавычек), SHA-256 provenance (FIPS 180-4).
- Tests/OverlayManagerTests.cpp — ровно 12 assertions.
- Tests/SecureSkinImportTests.cpp — ровно 34 assertions (8 SHA + 16 path + 10 SVG).
- tools/validate_skin_usage.py — G3.5-валидатор usage.json (21 канонический
  токен, hex/rgb в usage запрещены) + fixtures позитив/негатив.
- tools/validate_soundbank.py — закрыта дыра CI-контракта: build.yml вызывает
  этот валидатор, ранее он отсутствовал в дереве. Реализован по политике
  SOUNDBANK/policy.json; гейт G5 на пересобранном дереве PASS.
- CMakeLists.txt — добавлены таргеты ppgu_overlay_contracts,
  ppgu_skin_import_contracts и два add_test.

**Вне-CI верификация (evidence, не замена CI):**
- SHA-256: block_ref-арифметика сверена с независимым эталоном (hashlib):
  4 вектора FIPS 180-4 + свип длин 0-259 — 264/264 MATCH. Найденный в
  процессе дефект касался кортежного стиля зеркала, а не C++-кода; в
  production пошёл только верифицированный последовательный стиль.
- Path-политика зеркало: 16/16. SVG-политика зеркало: 16/16 (включая
  onclick=, onload=, одинарные кавычки, href=https://, bare href).
- Валидатор G3.5 запущен реально: позитив PASS (rc=0), негатив FAIL (rc=1,
  hex-литерал + неизвестный токен).

**Исправление по журналу (обязательное):** digest 56-байтного FIPS-вектора:
корректное значение —
`248d6a61d20638b8e5c026930c3e6039a33ce45964ff2167f6ecedd419db06c1`.
Значения `2ff100b3...c25301b` и `...5ce9467e10a37fa...` из прежних записей
ОШИБОЧНЫ и не должны копироваться. Аналогично: SecureSkinImportTests —
ровно 34 assertions (не 33); реестр тем — 52 темы с default skin-052
(значение «102 темы» — дефект прежней документации, закрыт).

**Non-goals / границы:** DSP, audio callback, sampler, MIDI, APVTS
IDs/semantics, presets, SoundBank, skin inventory и schema не менялись.
OverlayManager в этой записи НЕ интегрирован в PPGUMultiWindowWorkspace —
интеграция — следующий отдельный scoped-этап.

**Следующий шаг:** интеграция OverlayManager в PPGUMultiWindowWorkspace
(Source/UI/), затем production-обвязка juce::ZipFile ->
SecureSkinImport::Entry. Каждый этап — отдельный commit с полным exact-SHA
гейтом.
