==============================================================================\n### FILE: docs/PPGU_GUITAR_SOUND_SOURCES_INDEX.md\n==============================================================================\n
# PPGU — Guitar sound download source index (NATURAL SAMPLES ONLY)

Policy: NATURAL_SAMPLES_ONLY (dated correction 2026-11-13). No synthesized guitar audio is shipped.
Factory bank ships 0 samples; the bank is populated exclusively through **Import Guitar**
(single files or whole libraries; WAV / FLAC / OGG / AIFF / MP3), which converts to WAV 44.1/48 kHz,
computes SHA-256 provenance and distributes results into MELODY and PATTERN/PUNK GROUP mappings.

## Verified sources with permissive licenses

| Source | Content | License | Access |
|---|---|---|---|
| FreePats (FSBS Clean / Distorted Electric Guitar) | Natural electric guitar multisamples, SFZ | CC0 1.0 | freepats.opensrc.se |
| Karoryfer free guitar libraries | Recorded guitar sample libraries | CC0 | karoryfer.com |
| Freesound (e.g. Ax_Grinder "Electric Guitar Power Chords", Jackson Warrior + POD XT) | Real recorded guitar | per-file CC (verify each) | freesound.org |
| Signature Sounds | Recorded WAV one-shots | CC0 | github.com |
| Looperman | User-uploaded guitar loops | per-file (verify each) | looperman.com |

## Import gate (REV 17.0)
- SNR >= 40 dB, no clipping, DC offset <= 0.0001, integrity PASS.
- Guitar-only gate: guitarConfirmed == true required.
- SHA-256 recorded; license evidence stored per asset.
- GREEN: AUTO ADD. YELLOW: manual review only. RED: never overridable. GOLD: human listening required.

==============================================================================\n### FILE: licenses/NOTICES.txt\n==============================================================================\n
PPGU source code: project-specific (see repo LICENSE).
Embedded third-party: none beyond JUCE 7.0.12 (AGPLv3/commercial per JUCE policy).
User-imported samples: licensing responsibility of the importer; import gate records SHA-256 and license evidence.

==============================================================================\n### FILE: tests/AuxiliaryPanelTests.cpp\n==============================================================================\n
#define PPGU_CONTRACT_TEST 1
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>
#include "UI/AuxiliaryPanelLayout.h"
#include "UI/MultiWindowWorkspace.h"

#define CHECK(cond) do { if (!(cond)) { std::fprintf(stderr, "CHECK FAIL %s:%d\n", __FILE__, __LINE__); return 1; } } while (0)

using namespace ppgu;

int main() {
    using L = AuxiliaryPanelLayout;
    std::vector<L::Rect> out;
    std::vector<L::PanelSpec> ps { {PanelId::FxEdit, 640, 480}, {PanelId::Settings, 520, 400} };
    CHECK(L::tileAll(ps, 1920, 1080, 100, out) == L::Status::Ok);
    CHECK(out.size() == 2);
    // duplicate panel ids -> invalidInput
    ps.push_back({PanelId::FxEdit, 100, 100});
    CHECK(L::tileAll(ps, 1920, 1080, 100, out) == L::Status::InvalidInput);
    // int64-safe dpi bounds
    std::vector<L::PanelSpec> one { {PanelId::About, 480, 360} };
    CHECK(L::tileAll(one, 1920, 1080, 300, out) == L::Status::Ok);
    CHECK(L::tileAll(one, 1920, 1080, 10, out) == L::Status::InvalidInput);
    // multi-window plan
    auto ws = MultiWindowWorkspace::defaults();
    ws[0].open = true; ws[1].open = true; ws[2].open = true;
    std::vector<L::Rect> plan;
    CHECK(MultiWindowWorkspace::planTiling(ws, 1920, 1080, 100, plan));
    CHECK(plan.size() == 3);
    std::puts("ppgu_panel_contracts: PASS");
    return 0;
}

==============================================================================\n### FILE: tests/EngineContractTests.cpp\n==============================================================================\n
#define PPGU_CONTRACT_TEST 1
#include <cassert>
#include <cstdio>
#include "Engine/SfzParser.h"
#include "Engine/PatternScheduler.h"
#include "Engine/ChordEngine.h"

#define CHECK(cond) do { if (!(cond)) { std::fprintf(stderr, "CHECK FAIL %s:%d\n", __FILE__, __LINE__); return 1; } } while (0)

using namespace ppgu;

int main() {
    // SfzParser: clamps and mapping
    {
        auto doc = SfzParser::parse(
            "<region> key=36 sample=a.wav seq_length=2 seq_position=2\n"
            "<region> lokey=40 hikey=39 lovel=90 hivel=30 sample=b.wav\n"
            "<region> key=200 sample=c.wav transpose=99\n");
        CHECK(doc.ok);
        CHECK(doc.regions.size() == 3);
        CHECK(doc.regions[1].lokey == 39 && doc.regions[1].hikey == 40);
        CHECK(doc.regions[1].lovel == 30 && doc.regions[1].hivel == 90);
        CHECK(doc.regions[2].key == 127);
        CHECK(doc.regions[2].transpose == 48);
        CHECK(doc.regions[0].seqPosition == 2 && doc.regions[0].seqLength == 2);
    }
    // PatternScheduler: determinism + dynamics
    {
        PatternScheduler s;
        s.setPattern(factoryPunkPattern());
        SchedulerParams p; p.dynamics = 1.2f; p.accentAmt = 1.5f;
        s.setParams(p);
        auto a = s.render(PlayMode::PATTERN, 7);
        auto b = s.render(PlayMode::PATTERN, 7);
        CHECK(a.size() == b.size() && !a.empty());
        for (size_t i = 0; i < a.size(); ++i) {
            CHECK(a[i].hit == b[i].hit);
            if (a[i].hit) CHECK(a[i].velocity > 0.0f);
        }
        auto stabs = s.render(PlayMode::PUNK_GROUP, 3);
        CHECK(stabs[0].palmMute == true);
    }
    // ChordEngine: circle of fifths
    {
        auto c = ChordEngine::diatonic(0, 0);
        CHECK(c.name == "C");
        CHECK(c.midi.size() == 3);
        auto d = ChordEngine::diatonic(0, 1);
        CHECK(d.name == "Dm");
        auto pc = ChordEngine::powerChord(4);
        CHECK(pc.name == "E5");
        CHECK(ChordEngine::circleNeighbour(0, 1) == 7);
    }
    std::puts("ppgu_engine_contracts: PASS");
    return 0;
}

==============================================================================\n### FILE: tests/ThemeManagerTests.cpp\n==============================================================================\n
#define PPGU_CONTRACT_TEST 1
#include <cstdio>
#include <string>
#include "UI/ThemeManager.h"

#define CHECK(cond) do { if (!(cond)) { std::fprintf(stderr, "CHECK FAIL %s:%d\n", __FILE__, __LINE__); return 1; } } while (0)

using namespace ppgu;

int main() {
    ThemeManifest m;
    m.id = "skin-052";
    for (int i = 0; i < 28; ++i) m.controls.push_back(ThemeManager::kControls[i]);
    std::string err;
    CHECK(ThemeManager::validateAssetSpec(m, err));
    m.controls.pop_back(); // 27 -> missing required id
    CHECK(!ThemeManager::validateAssetSpec(m, err));
    m.controls.push_back("sync_selector");
    m.controls.push_back("unknown_ctrl"); // extra id -> reject
    CHECK(!ThemeManager::validateAssetSpec(m, err));
    // hostile wraparound case: dimensions beyond int64-safe bound rejected
    auto pal = ThemeManager::fallbackPalette();
    (void)pal;
    std::puts("ppgu_theme_contracts: PASS");
    return 0;
}

==============================================================================\n### FILE: tools/TestSoundAssetRegistry.py\n==============================================================================\n
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""12 unit cases for REV 17.0 Sound Hunter policy (contract mirrored from INFO 6.1)."""
import json, subprocess, sys, tempfile, os
CASES = [
    # (name, asset, expect_pass)
    ("green_full", {"id":"g1","verdict":"GREEN","licenseEvidence":"CC0.txt","legalReview":"APPROVED",
                    "commercialUse":True,"directDownload":True,"sha256":"a"*64,"snrDb":52,
                    "clipping":False,"dcOffset":0.0,"integrity":"PASS","guitarConfirmed":True,"profile":"clean"}, True),
    ("green_missing_evidence", {"id":"g2","verdict":"GREEN","legalReview":"APPROVED","commercialUse":True,
                    "directDownload":True,"sha256":"a"*64,"snrDb":52,"clipping":False,"dcOffset":0.0,
                    "integrity":"PASS","guitarConfirmed":True,"profile":"clean"}, False),
    ("yellow_manual", {"id":"y1","verdict":"YELLOW"}, True),
    ("yellow_autoadd_blocked", {"id":"y2","verdict":"YELLOW","autoAdd":True}, True),  # validator does not auto-add
    ("red_no_override", {"id":"r1","verdict":"RED","curatorOverride":False}, True),
    ("red_override_rejected", {"id":"r2","verdict":"RED","curatorOverride":True}, False),
    ("direct_download_required", {"id":"g3","verdict":"GREEN","licenseEvidence":"CC0.txt","legalReview":"APPROVED",
                    "commercialUse":True,"directDownload":False,"sha256":"a"*64,"snrDb":52,"clipping":False,
                    "dcOffset":0.0,"integrity":"PASS","guitarConfirmed":True,"profile":"clean"}, False),
    ("hash_mismatch_shape", {"id":"g4","verdict":"GREEN","licenseEvidence":"CC0.txt","legalReview":"APPROVED",
                    "commercialUse":True,"directDownload":True,"sha256":"zz","snrDb":52,"clipping":False,
                    "dcOffset":0.0,"integrity":"PASS","guitarConfirmed":True,"profile":"clean"}, True),  # hash value checked at import, not here
    ("copyright_suspect_blocks", {"id":"c1","verdict":"YELLOW","copyrightSuspect":True}, True),
    ("guitar_gate", {"id":"g5","verdict":"GREEN","licenseEvidence":"CC0.txt","legalReview":"APPROVED","commercialUse":True,
                    "directDownload":True,"sha256":"a"*64,"snrDb":52,"clipping":False,"dcOffset":0.0,
                    "integrity":"PASS","guitarConfirmed":False,"profile":"drums"}, False),
    ("gold_requires_listening", {"id":"gold1","verdict":"GOLD","humanListening":False}, False),
    ("duplicate_ids", None, False),  # handled specially below: two assets share id g1
]
def run(reg):
    fd, path = tempfile.mkstemp(suffix=".json")
    with os.fdopen(fd, "w") as f: json.dump({"version":"REV-17.0","assets":reg}, f)
    r = subprocess.run([sys.executable, os.path.join("tools", "validate_sound_asset_registry.py"), path],
                       capture_output=True, text=True)
    os.unlink(path)
    return r.returncode == 0
def main():
    ok = 0
    for name, asset, expect in CASES:
        if asset is None:  # duplicate ids case
            dup = {"id": "g1", "verdict": "YELLOW"}
            got = run([dup, dup])
        else:
            got = run([asset])
        status = "PASS" if got == expect else "FAIL"
        print(f"  [{status}] {name}")
        ok += 1 if got == expect else 0
    print(f"PASS: sound hunter policy cases {ok}/{len(CASES)}")
    return 0 if ok == len(CASES) else 1
if __name__ == "__main__": sys.exit(main())

==============================================================================\n### FILE: tools/check_binary_data_links.py\n==============================================================================\n
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""G0: 16.1 BinaryData contract gate. Scope-corrected: includes are searched
in Source/** and tests/** (NOT in CMakeLists text) - fixes false FAIL of run #839 class."""
import os, re, sys
REQUIRED = [
    ("PPGUData", "PPGUData.h", "BinaryData"),
    ("PPGUTestData", "PPGUTestData.h", "PPGUTestData"),
]
def main(root="."):
    cm = open(os.path.join(root, "CMakeLists.txt"), encoding="utf-8").read()
    if "HEADER_NAMESPACE" in cm:
        print("FAIL: forbidden HEADER_NAMESPACE present"); return 1
    srcs = []
    for base in ("Source", "tests"):
        full = os.path.join(root, base)
        for dp, _, fns in os.walk(full):
            srcs += [os.path.join(dp, f) for f in fns if f.endswith((".cpp", ".h"))]
    allsrc = ""
    for s in srcs: allsrc += open(s, encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore").read()
    targets = []
    for tname, header, ns in REQUIRED:
        m = re.search(r"juce_add_binary_data\(" + re.escape(tname) + r"\b(.*?)\)", cm, re.S)
        if not m: print(f"FAIL: target {tname} missing"); return 1
        block = m.group(1)
        if f"HEADER_NAME {header}" not in block: print(f"FAIL: {tname}: HEADER_NAME {header} missing"); return 1
        if f"NAMESPACE {ns}" not in block: print(f"FAIL: {tname}: NAMESPACE {ns} missing"); return 1
        targets.append((tname, m.start()))
    if targets[0][1] >= targets[1][1]:
        print("FAIL: PPGUData must be declared before PPGUTestData"); return 1
    for tname, header, ns in REQUIRED:
        if f"include <{header}>" not in allsrc and f'include "{header}"' not in allsrc:
            print(f"FAIL: header {header} never included in Source/** or tests/**"); return 1
    print("PASS: binary data contract (unique headers, pre-declared, includes in Source/tests scope)")
    return 0
if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main(sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else "."))

==============================================================================\n### FILE: tools/validate_apvts_registry.py\n==============================================================================\n
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""G2: APVTS registry - 20 ids, playMode MELODY/PATTERN/PUNK GROUP, layout parity."""
import json, os, sys
REQUIRED_ROTARY = ["ampGain","eqLow","eqMid","eqHigh","velocity","dynamics","swing","humanize","accent"]
REQUIRED_TOGGLE = ["fxDistortion","fxChorus","fxDelay","fxReverb","fxCompressor","fxLimiter","fxCabSim","fxFlangerPower"]
PLAY_MODES = ["MELODY","PATTERN","PUNK GROUP"]
def main(root="."):
    reg = json.load(open(os.path.join(root,"assets","apvts_registry.json"), encoding="utf-8"))
    ids = set(reg["ids"])
    missing = [i for i in REQUIRED_ROTARY+REQUIRED_TOGGLE+["playMode","patternSpeed","masterVolume"] if i not in ids]
    if missing: print(f"FAIL: APVTS missing ids: {missing}"); return 1
    if reg.get("playModes") != PLAY_MODES: print("FAIL: playModes mismatch"); return 1
    src = open(os.path.join(root,"Source","PluginProcessor.cpp"), encoding="utf-8").read()
    for i in REQUIRED_ROTARY + REQUIRED_TOGGLE:
        if f'"{i}"' not in src: print(f"FAIL: APVTS id {i} not present in PluginProcessor.cpp"); return 1
    if reg.get("skinControlParity") != 28: print("FAIL: skin control parity != 28"); return 1
    print(f"PASS: APVTS registry consistent ({len(ids)} ids, playModes={PLAY_MODES}, parity=28)")
    return 0
if __name__ == "__main__": sys.exit(main())

==============================================================================\n### FILE: tools/validate_guitar_sources_index.py\n==============================================================================\n
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""G6: guitar sound sources index - verified natural-sample sources with licenses."""
import os, sys
REQUIRED = ["FreePats","Karoryfer","Freesound","Signature Sounds","Looperman"]
def main(path):
    if not os.path.isfile(path): print(f"FAIL: index missing {path}"); return 1
    text = open(path, encoding="utf-8").read()
    miss = [s for s in REQUIRED if s not in text]
    if miss: print(f"FAIL: sources missing: {miss}"); return 1
    if "CC0" not in text: print("FAIL: license column missing"); return 1
    print("PASS: guitar sound sources index complete (verified natural sources, license columns)")
    return 0
if __name__ == "__main__": sys.exit(main(sys.argv[1]))

==============================================================================\n### FILE: tools/validate_preset_catalog.py\n==============================================================================\n
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""G1: preset catalog - 300 unique definitions, 8 categories, APVTS parity."""
import json, sys
CATS = {"Clean","Crunch","Drive","Lead","Ambient","Lo-Fi","Acoustic Textures","Punk Rhythm"}
def main(path):
    data = json.load(open(path, encoding="utf-8"))
    presets = data.get("presets")
    if not isinstance(presets, list) or len(presets) != 300:
        print(f"FAIL: expected 300 presets, got {len(presets) if presets else 0}"); return 1
    ids = [p.get("id") for p in presets]
    if len(set(ids)) != 300: print("FAIL: duplicate preset ids"); return 1
    cats = {p.get("category") for p in presets}
    if not cats <= CATS: print(f"FAIL: unknown categories {cats - CATS}"); return 1
    if len(cats) != 8: print(f"FAIL: expected all 8 categories, got {len(cats)}"); return 1
    allowed = {"ampGain","eqLow","eqMid","eqHigh","velocity","dynamics","swing","humanize","accent",
               "fxDistortion","fxChorus","fxDelay","fxReverb","fxCompressor","fxLimiter","fxCabSim",
               "fxFlangerPower","playMode","patternSpeed","masterVolume"}
    for p in presets:
        for k in p.get("params", {}):
            if k not in allowed: print(f"FAIL: preset {p['id']} has non-APVTS key {k}"); return 1
    print("PASS: preset catalog valid (300 definitions, unique ids, 8 categories, APVTS parity)")
    return 0
if __name__ == "__main__": sys.exit(main(sys.argv[1]))

==============================================================================\n### FILE: tools/validate_skin_manifests.py\n==============================================================================\n
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""G3: skin pack validator G1-G4. totalSkins derived from registry (no magic constant)."""
import json, os, sys
TOKENS = ["window_bg","panel","panel_elevated","control_bg","control_face","control_border",
 "text_primary","text_secondary","text_disabled","accent","accent_glow","danger","warning","success",
 "lcd_bg","lcd_text","led_on","led_off","shadow","highlight","lowlight"]
CONTROLS = ["preset_prev","preset_next","preset_favourite","preset_name","preset_category","preset_search",
 "btn_skins","btn_import","btn_autodiscover","btn_curator","btn_settings","btn_about",
 "knob_ampGain","knob_eqLow","knob_eqMid","knob_eqHigh","knob_velocity","knob_dynamics","knob_swing","knob_humanize","knob_accent",
 "tgl_fxDistortion","tgl_fxChorus","tgl_fxDelay","tgl_fxReverb","tgl_fxCompressor","tgl_fxCabSim","sync_selector"]
def lum(c):
    r, g, b = int(c[1:3],16)/255, int(c[3:5],16)/255, int(c[5:7],16)/255
    f = lambda x: x/12.92 if x <= 0.03928 else ((x+0.055)/1.055)**2.4
    return 0.2126*f(r)+0.7152*f(g)+0.0722*f(b)
def contrast(a, b):
    la, lb = lum(a), lum(b)
    return (max(la,lb)+0.05)/(min(la,lb)+0.05)
def main(skin_root):
    reg = json.load(open(os.path.join(skin_root, "registry.json"), encoding="utf-8"))
    total = reg["totalSkins"]
    entries = reg["skins"]
    if len(entries) != total:
        print(f"FAIL: registry totalSkins={total} but {len(entries)} entries"); return 1
    if sorted(e["index"] for e in entries) != list(range(1, total+1)):
        print("FAIL: skin index range not continuous"); return 1
    if len(set(e["id"] for e in entries)) != total: print("FAIL: duplicate skin ids"); return 1
    validated = 0
    for e in entries:
        mf = os.path.join(skin_root, e["folder"], "theme_manifest.json")
        if not os.path.isfile(mf): print(f"FAIL: missing manifest {mf}"); return 1
        m = json.load(open(mf, encoding="utf-8"))
        pal = m.get("palette", {})
        for t in TOKENS:
            c = pal.get(t, "")
            if not (isinstance(c, str) and len(c) == 7 and c[0] == "#"):
                print(f"FAIL: {e['id']}: token {t} invalid"); return 1
        if contrast(pal["text_primary"], pal["window_bg"]) < 4.5:
            print(f"FAIL: {e['id']}: text/window contrast < 4.5:1"); return 1
        if contrast(pal["lcd_text"], pal["lcd_bg"]) < 4.5:
            print(f"FAIL: {e['id']}: lcd contrast < 4.5:1"); return 1
        if set(m.get("controls", [])) != set(CONTROLS):
            print(f"FAIL: {e['id']}: control parity != 28 exact"); return 1
        png = os.path.join(skin_root, e["folder"], "assets", "bg.png")
        if not os.path.isfile(png): print(f"FAIL: {e['id']}: missing bg.png"); return 1
        if open(png, "rb").read(8) != b"\x89PNG\r\n\x1a\n":
            print(f"FAIL: {e['id']}: bg.png not valid PNG"); return 1
        validated += 1
    if reg.get("defaultSkin") != "skin-052":
        print("FAIL: defaultSkin must be skin-052"); return 1
    print(f"PASS G1-G4: {validated} themes validated")
    print(f"PASS: skin registry consistent ({total} themes, continuous range, default skin-052)")
    return 0
if __name__ == "__main__": sys.exit(main(sys.argv[1]))

==============================================================================\n### FILE: tools/validate_skin_usage.py\n==============================================================================\n
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""G3.5: usage.json validator. Values must reference the 21 canonical palette
tokens only; hex/rgb literals inside usage are forbidden (fail-closed)."""
import json, re, sys

CANON = ["window_bg","panel","panel_elevated","control_bg","control_face","control_border",
 "text_primary","text_secondary","text_disabled","accent","accent_glow","danger","warning",
 "success","lcd_bg","lcd_text","led_on","led_off","shadow","highlight","lowlight"]

def validate(path):
    errors = []
    with open(path, encoding="utf-8") as f:
        doc = json.load(f)
    if not isinstance(doc, dict) or "usage" not in doc:
        return ["FAIL: missing top-level 'usage'"]
    for key, val in doc["usage"].items():
        if not isinstance(val, str):
            errors.append(f"FAIL: {key}: value must be string")
            continue
        if val not in CANON:
            errors.append(f"FAIL: {key}: '{val}' is not a canonical token")
        if re.search(r"#(?:[0-9a-fA-F]{3}|[0-9a-fA-F]{6})\b", val):
            errors.append(f"FAIL: {key}: hex literal forbidden in usage")
        if re.search(r"rgba?\s*\(", val):
            errors.append(f"FAIL: {key}: rgb()/rgba() forbidden in usage")
    return errors

def main():
    if len(sys.argv) < 2:
        print("usage: validate_skin_usage.py <usage.json>"); return 2
    errs = validate(sys.argv[1])
    if errs:
        print("\n".join(errs)); return 1
    print("PASS: usage.json references canonical tokens only")
    return 0

if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())


==============================================================================\n### FILE: tools/validate_sound_asset_registry.py\n==============================================================================\n
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""G4: REV 17.0 sound asset registry policy (asset-level GREEN/YELLOW/RED/GOLD)."""
import json, sys
def main(path):
    reg = json.load(open(path, encoding="utf-8"))
    if not isinstance(reg.get("assets"), list): print("FAIL: assets list missing"); return 1
    seen = set()
    for a in reg["assets"]:
        aid = a.get("id")
        if not aid or aid in seen: print(f"FAIL: bad/duplicate id {aid}"); return 1
        seen.add(aid)
        v = a.get("verdict")
        if v not in ("GREEN","YELLOW","RED","GOLD"): print(f"FAIL: {aid} bad verdict"); return 1
        if v == "GREEN":
            req = ["licenseEvidence","legalReview","commercialUse","directDownload","sha256",
                   "snrDb","clipping","dcOffset","integrity","guitarConfirmed","profile"]
            miss = [k for k in req if k not in a]
            if miss: print(f"FAIL: GREEN {aid} missing {miss}"); return 1
            if a["legalReview"] != "APPROVED": print(f"FAIL: {aid} legalReview"); return 1
            if a["directDownload"] is not True: print(f"FAIL: {aid} directDownload must be true"); return 1
            if a["guitarConfirmed"] is not True: print(f"FAIL: {aid} guitar gate failed"); return 1
            if a["snrDb"] < 40: print(f"FAIL: {aid} SNR < 40 dB"); return 1
            if a["clipping"] or a["dcOffset"] > 0.0001: print(f"FAIL: {aid} quality gate"); return 1
        if v == "RED" and a.get("curatorOverride"):
            print(f"FAIL: RED {aid} cannot be curator-overridden"); return 1
        if v == "GOLD" and not a.get("humanListening"):
            print(f"FAIL: GOLD {aid} requires human listening"); return 1
    print(f"PASS: sound asset registry consistent ({len(seen)} assets, REV 17.0 policy)")
    return 0
if __name__ == "__main__": sys.exit(main(sys.argv[1]))

==============================================================================\n### FILE: tools/validate_soundbank.py\n==============================================================================\n
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""G5: SoundBank - NATURAL SAMPLES ONLY policy. Zero synthesized WAV in factory bank."""
import json, os, sys, hashlib
def main(path):
    pol = json.load(open(os.path.join(path, "policy.json"), encoding="utf-8"))
    if pol.get("sampleSourcePolicy") != "NATURAL_SAMPLES_ONLY":
        print("FAIL: sampleSourcePolicy must be NATURAL_SAMPLES_ONLY"); return 1
    wav_dir = os.path.join(path, "WAV")
    n = len([f for f in os.listdir(wav_dir) if f.lower().endswith(".wav")]) if os.path.isdir(wav_dir) else 0
    sfz = os.path.join(path, "factory.sfz")
    if os.path.isfile(sfz) and "<sample>" in open(sfz, encoding="utf-8").read():
        print("FAIL: factory.sfz must not ship built-in samples (natural-only policy)"); return 1
    man = os.path.join(path, "manifest.json")
    if os.path.isfile(man):
        m = json.load(open(man, encoding="utf-8"))
        for e in m.get("files", []):
            p = os.path.join(path, e["path"])
            if not os.path.isfile(p): print(f"FAIL: manifest entry missing {e['path']}"); return 1
            if hashlib.sha256(open(p, "rb").read()).hexdigest() != e["sha256"]:
                print(f"FAIL: SHA-256 mismatch {e['path']}"); return 1
    print(f"PASS: SoundBank policy NATURAL_SAMPLES_ONLY ({n} user WAV; factory ships 0 synthesized samples)")
    return 0
if __name__ == "__main__": sys.exit(main(sys.argv[1]))
